# apps/accounts/services.py
from datetime import timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from django.conf import settings
from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework.authtoken.models import Token

from apps.accounts.models import (
    AuthOtp,
    CoordinatorProfile,
    User,
    UserCharityInterest,
    UserVerificationImage,
)
from apps.audit.services import log_action
from apps.common.constants import (
    ACCOUNT_CLOSED,
    COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
    COORDINATOR_PENDING,
    COORDINATOR_REVOKED,
    COORDINATOR_SUSPENDED,
    IMAGE_DRIVER_LICENSE,
    IMAGE_ID_LICENSE,
    IMAGE_MOCK_SELFIE,
    OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS,
    OTP_TTL_SECONDS,
    VERIFICATION_NONE,
    VERIFICATION_PENDING,
    VERIFICATION_REJECTED,
    VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
)
from apps.common.exceptions import (
    AccountClosedError,
    DomainError,
    InvalidOtpError,
    TooManyAttemptsError,
    VerificationRequiredError,
)
from apps.common.permissions import ensure_active_account, ensure_admin
from apps.common.security import (
    compute_phone_hash,
    encrypt_phone,
    generate_otp_code,
    hash_otp,
    normalize_phone,
)
from apps.common.validators import validate_and_transcode_jpeg
from apps.events.models import CharityField


def request_otp(phone: str, purpose: str = "login") -> dict:
    raw_phone = normalize_phone(phone)
    phone_hash = compute_phone_hash(raw_phone)

    # Check if existing account is closed
    existing_user = User.objects.filter(phone_hash=phone_hash).first()
    if existing_user and existing_user.account_status == ACCOUNT_CLOSED:
        raise AccountClosedError()

    # Generate OTP code (or fixed dev code if configured)
    if settings.DEBUG and getattr(settings, "DEV_FIXED_OTP", None):
        code = str(settings.DEV_FIXED_OTP)
    else:
        code = generate_otp_code()

    code_hash = hash_otp(code)
    expires_at = timezone.now() + timedelta(seconds=OTP_TTL_SECONDS)

    AuthOtp.objects.create(
        phone_hash=phone_hash,
        code_hash=code_hash,
        purpose=purpose,
        expires_at=expires_at,
    )

    response = {"status": "sent"}
    # In DEBUG or test environments, expose code for easy testing/local dev
    if settings.DEBUG or getattr(settings, "TESTING", False):
        response["dev_code"] = code

    return response


def verify_otp(phone: str, code: str, purpose: str = "login") -> tuple[User, Token, bool]:
    raw_phone = normalize_phone(phone)
    phone_hash = compute_phone_hash(raw_phone)

    otp = (
        AuthOtp.objects.filter(
            phone_hash=phone_hash,
            purpose=purpose,
            consumed_at__isnull=True,
        )
        .order_by("-created_at")
        .first()
    )

    if not otp or otp.expires_at <= timezone.now():
        raise InvalidOtpError("Invalid or expired OTP.")

    if otp.attempt_count >= OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS:
        raise TooManyAttemptsError("Too many attempts. Request a new OTP.")

    # Increment attempt count and save immediately
    otp.attempt_count += 1
    otp.save(update_fields=["attempt_count"])

    # Validate OTP hash
    is_valid = hash_otp(code) == otp.code_hash
    if not is_valid and settings.DEBUG and getattr(settings, "DEV_FIXED_OTP", None):
        is_valid = str(code) == str(settings.DEV_FIXED_OTP)

    if not is_valid:
        raise InvalidOtpError("Invalid OTP.")

    with transaction.atomic():
        # Mark consumed
        otp.consumed_at = timezone.now()
        otp.save(update_fields=["consumed_at"])

        # Get or create User
        created = False
        user = User.objects.filter(phone_hash=phone_hash).first()
        if not user:
            display_name = f"Volunteer {raw_phone[-4:]}" if len(raw_phone) >= 4 else "Volunteer"
            user = User.objects.create(
                phone_hash=phone_hash,
                phone_enc=encrypt_phone(raw_phone),
                display_name=display_name,
            )
            created = True

        # Validate account status (raises AccountClosedError or AccountBlockedError)
        ensure_active_account(user)

        # Issue or retrieve token
        token, _ = Token.objects.get_or_create(user=user)

        # Log login action
        log_action(actor=user, action="login", entity_type="user", entity_id=user.id)

    return (user, token, created)


def issue_token_for_user(user: User) -> Token:
    ensure_active_account(user)
    token, _ = Token.objects.get_or_create(user=user)
    return token


def update_profile(user: User, data: dict) -> User:
    """
    Update volunteer profile.
    Disallows updating is_admin, account_status, verification_status directly.
    """
    forbidden_fields = {"is_admin", "account_status", "verification_status", "blocked_until", "id", "phone_hash", "phone_enc"}
    attempted_forbidden = forbidden_fields.intersection(data.keys())
    if attempted_forbidden:
        raise DomainError(
            f"Cannot update restricted field(s): {', '.join(sorted(attempted_forbidden))}",
            code="field_not_allowed",
        )

    clean_data = dict(data)
    with transaction.atomic():
        if "emergency_contact_phone" in clean_data:
            raw_contact_phone = clean_data.pop("emergency_contact_phone")
            if raw_contact_phone:
                clean_data["emergency_contact_phone_enc"] = encrypt_phone(normalize_phone(raw_contact_phone))
            else:
                clean_data["emergency_contact_phone_enc"] = None

        for field, value in clean_data.items():
            if hasattr(user, field):
                setattr(user, field, value)

        user.clean()
        user.save()

        log_action(
            actor=user,
            action="profile_update",
            entity_type="user",
            entity_id=user.id,
            after_data={k: v for k, v in clean_data.items() if not k.endswith("_enc")},
        )

    return user


def set_charity_interests(user: User, codes: list[str]) -> None:
    """
    Atomically replace UserCharityInterest records for user.
    """
    with transaction.atomic():
        valid_fields = list(CharityField.objects.filter(code__in=codes, active=True))
        UserCharityInterest.objects.filter(user=user).delete()
        UserCharityInterest.objects.bulk_create(
            [UserCharityInterest(user=user, field_code=field) for field in valid_fields]
        )
        log_action(
            actor=user,
            action="set_charity_interests",
            entity_type="user",
            entity_id=user.id,
            after_data={"codes": codes},
        )


def add_verification_image(user: User, image_type: str, uploaded_file) -> UserVerificationImage:
    """
    Validate and transcode uploaded image to JPEG.
    Deactivates previous images of the same image_type.
    Creates new UserVerificationImage and recomputes user verification status.
    """
    valid_types = {IMAGE_MOCK_SELFIE, IMAGE_ID_LICENSE, IMAGE_DRIVER_LICENSE}
    if image_type not in valid_types:
        raise DomainError(f"Invalid image type: {image_type}", code="invalid_image_type")

    dest_dir = Path(settings.MEDIA_ROOT) / "verification_images"
    rel_path, sha256_hex = validate_and_transcode_jpeg(uploaded_file, dest_dir)

    with transaction.atomic():
        # Deactivate old current images of the same image_type
        UserVerificationImage.objects.filter(
            user=user,
            image_type=image_type,
            is_current=True,
        ).update(is_current=False)

        new_image = UserVerificationImage.objects.create(
            user=user,
            image_type=image_type,
            image_path=rel_path,
            file_format="jpeg",
            file_sha256=sha256_hex,
            status=VERIFICATION_PENDING,
            is_current=True,
        )

        recompute_verification_status(user)

        log_action(
            actor=user,
            action="upload_verification_image",
            entity_type="verification_image",
            entity_id=new_image.id,
            after_data={"image_type": image_type, "file_sha256": sha256_hex},
        )

    return new_image


def review_verification_image(
    actor: User,
    image: UserVerificationImage,
    status: str,
    note: str = "",
) -> UserVerificationImage:
    """
    Admin review of user verification image.
    Updates status, reviewer, and recomputes verification status of target user.
    """
    ensure_admin(actor)

    valid_statuses = {"approved", "verified", VERIFICATION_VERIFIED, VERIFICATION_REJECTED}
    if status not in valid_statuses:
        raise DomainError(f"Invalid review status: {status}", code="invalid_status")

    db_status = "approved" if status in ("approved", "verified", VERIFICATION_VERIFIED) else VERIFICATION_REJECTED

    with transaction.atomic():
        image.status = db_status
        image.reviewed_by = actor
        image.reviewed_at = timezone.now()
        image.note = note
        image.save(update_fields=["status", "reviewed_by", "reviewed_at", "note"])

        recompute_verification_status(image.user)

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action=f"review_image_{db_status}",
            entity_type="verification_image",
            entity_id=image.id,
            after_data={"status": db_status, "note": note},
        )

    return image


def recompute_verification_status(user: User) -> None:
    """
    Recompute user's verification_status based on current verification images.
    - If any current image rejected -> rejected
    - Else if approved mock_selfie AND (approved id_license OR approved driver_license) -> verified
    - Else if any current pending -> pending
    - Else -> none
    """
    current_images = list(UserVerificationImage.objects.filter(user=user, is_current=True))

    has_rejected = any(img.status in ("rejected", VERIFICATION_REJECTED) for img in current_images)
    has_pending = any(img.status in ("pending", VERIFICATION_PENDING) for img in current_images)
    has_mock = any(
        img.status in ("approved", "verified", VERIFICATION_VERIFIED) and img.image_type == IMAGE_MOCK_SELFIE
        for img in current_images
    )
    has_license = any(
        img.status in ("approved", "verified", VERIFICATION_VERIFIED)
        and img.image_type in (IMAGE_ID_LICENSE, IMAGE_DRIVER_LICENSE)
        for img in current_images
    )

    if has_rejected:
        new_status = VERIFICATION_REJECTED
    elif has_mock and has_license:
        new_status = VERIFICATION_VERIFIED
    elif has_pending:
        new_status = VERIFICATION_PENDING
    else:
        new_status = VERIFICATION_NONE

    user.verification_status = new_status
    user.save(update_fields=["verification_status"])


def apply_as_coordinator(user: User, notes: str = "") -> CoordinatorProfile:
    """
    Active user applies to become a coordinator.
    """
    ensure_active_account(user)

    with transaction.atomic():
        profile, created = CoordinatorProfile.objects.get_or_create(
            user=user,
            defaults={"status": COORDINATOR_PENDING, "notes": notes},
        )
        if not created and profile.status in (COORDINATOR_REVOKED, COORDINATOR_SUSPENDED):
            profile.status = COORDINATOR_PENDING
            if notes:
                profile.notes = notes
            profile.save(update_fields=["status", "notes"])

        log_action(
            actor=user,
            action="apply_coordinator",
            entity_type="coordinator_profile",
            entity_id=user.id,
        )

    return profile


def approve_coordinator(actor: User, profile: CoordinatorProfile) -> CoordinatorProfile:
    """
    Admin approves coordinator application.
    Requires user.verification_status == 'verified'.
    """
    ensure_admin(actor)

    profile.user.refresh_from_db()
    if profile.user.verification_status != VERIFICATION_VERIFIED:
        raise VerificationRequiredError(
            "User must be fully verified before coordinator status can be approved."
        )

    with transaction.atomic():
        profile.status = COORDINATOR_APPROVED
        profile.approved_by = actor
        profile.approved_at = timezone.now()
        profile.save(update_fields=["status", "approved_by", "approved_at"])

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="approve_coordinator",
            entity_type="coordinator_profile",
            entity_id=profile.user_id,
        )

    return profile


def suspend_coordinator(actor: User, profile: CoordinatorProfile) -> CoordinatorProfile:
    """
    Admin suspends an approved coordinator.
    """
    ensure_admin(actor)

    with transaction.atomic():
        profile.status = COORDINATOR_SUSPENDED
        profile.save(update_fields=["status"])

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="suspend_coordinator",
            entity_type="coordinator_profile",
            entity_id=profile.user_id,
        )

    return profile


def revoke_coordinator(actor: User, profile: CoordinatorProfile) -> CoordinatorProfile:
    """
    Admin revokes coordinator role permanently.
    """
    ensure_admin(actor)

    with transaction.atomic():
        profile.status = COORDINATOR_REVOKED
        profile.save(update_fields=["status"])

        log_action(
            actor=actor,
            action="revoke_coordinator",
            entity_type="coordinator_profile",
            entity_id=profile.user_id,
        )

    return profile

# apps/accounts/views.py
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.parsers import FormParser, JSONParser, MultiPartParser
from rest_framework.permissions import AllowAny, IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.accounts.models import CoordinatorProfile
from apps.accounts.serializers import (
    CharityInterestsUpdateSerializer,
    CoordinatorApplySerializer,
    CoordinatorProfileSerializer,
    OtpRequestSerializer,
    OtpVerifySerializer,
    UserProfileSerializer,
    VerificationImageSerializer,
    VerificationImageUploadSerializer,
)
from apps.accounts.services import (
    add_verification_image,
    apply_as_coordinator,
    request_otp,
    set_charity_interests,
    update_profile,
    verify_otp,
)
from apps.common.permissions import IsActiveAccount


class OtpRequestView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]

    def post(self, request):
        serializer = OtpRequestSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        res = request_otp(
            phone=serializer.validated_data["phone"],
            purpose=serializer.validated_data.get("purpose", "login"),
        )
        return Response(res, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class OtpVerifyView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]

    def post(self, request):
        serializer = OtpVerifySerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        user, token, created = verify_otp(
            phone=serializer.validated_data["phone"],
            code=serializer.validated_data["code"],
            purpose=serializer.validated_data.get("purpose", "login"),
        )
        return Response(
            {
                "token": token.key,
                "user": UserProfileSerializer(user).data,
                "created": created,
            },
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        )


class MeProfileView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def get(self, request):
        serializer = UserProfileSerializer(request.user)
        return Response(serializer.data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    def patch(self, request):
        # We pass request.data directly to update_profile so domain service detects forbidden field tampering
        data = request.data.dict() if hasattr(request.data, "dict") else dict(request.data)
        updated_user = update_profile(request.user, data)
        return Response(UserProfileSerializer(updated_user).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class MeCharityInterestsView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def get(self, request):
        interests = list(request.user.charity_interests.values_list("field_code_id", flat=True))
        return Response({"interests": interests}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    def put(self, request):
        serializer = CharityInterestsUpdateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        codes = serializer.validated_data["codes"]
        set_charity_interests(request.user, codes)
        return Response({"status": "ok", "interests": codes}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class MeVerificationImagesView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]
    parser_classes = [MultiPartParser, FormParser, JSONParser]

    def get(self, request):
        images = request.user.verification_images.all().order_by("-created_at")
        serializer = VerificationImageSerializer(images, many=True)
        return Response(serializer.data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    def post(self, request):
        serializer = VerificationImageUploadSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        new_image = add_verification_image(
            user=request.user,
            image_type=serializer.validated_data["image_type"],
            uploaded_file=serializer.validated_data["image"],
        )
        return Response(
            VerificationImageSerializer(new_image).data,
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )


class MeCoordinatorView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsActiveAccount]

    def get(self, request):
        profile = CoordinatorProfile.objects.filter(user=request.user).first()
        if not profile:
            return Response({"status": "none"}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
        return Response(CoordinatorProfileSerializer(profile).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    def post(self, request):
        serializer = CoordinatorApplySerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
        profile = apply_as_coordinator(
            user=request.user,
            notes=serializer.validated_data.get("notes", ""),
        )
        return Response(
            CoordinatorProfileSerializer(profile).data,
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        )

        "entity_type",
        "entity_id",

        return False

    def has_change_permission(self, request, obj=None):
        return False

# apps/audit/models.py
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import models
from django.db.models import Q


class ModerationAction(models.Model):
    USER_ACTION_TYPES = [
        "user_warning",
        "user_block",
        "user_unblock",
        "account_close",
        "account_reinstate",
    ]
    EVENT_ACTION_TYPES = [
        "event_delete",
        "event_restore",
        "event_cancel",
    ]
    FEEDBACK_ACTION_TYPES = [
        "feedback_hide",
    ]

    ACTION_TYPE_CHOICES = [
        ("user_warning", "User Warning"),
        ("user_block", "User Block"),
        ("user_unblock", "User Unblock"),
        ("account_close", "Account Close"),
        ("account_reinstate", "Account Reinstate"),
        ("event_delete", "Event Delete"),
        ("event_restore", "Event Restore"),
        ("event_cancel", "Event Cancel"),
        ("feedback_hide", "Feedback Hide"),
    ]

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    admin_user = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.PROTECT,
        related_name="performed_moderations",
    )
    target_user = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="moderation_records",
    )
    event = models.ForeignKey(
        "events.Event",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="moderation_records",
    )
    feedback = models.ForeignKey(
        "ops.Feedback",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="moderation_records",
    )

    action_type = models.CharField(max_length=30, choices=ACTION_TYPE_CHOICES)
    reason = models.TextField(null=True, blank=True)
    expires_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "moderation_actions"
        verbose_name = "Moderation Action"
        verbose_name_plural = "Moderation Actions"
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=(
                    Q(
                        action_type__in=[
                            "user_warning",
                            "user_block",
                            "user_unblock",
                            "account_close",
                            "account_reinstate",
                        ]
                    )
                    & Q(target_user__isnull=False)
                )
                | (
                    Q(
                        action_type__in=[
                            "event_delete",
                            "event_restore",
                            "event_cancel",
                        ]
                    )
                    & Q(event__isnull=False)
                )
                | (
                    Q(action_type="feedback_hide")
                    & Q(feedback__isnull=False)
                ),
                name="ck_moderation_subject",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(expires_at__isnull=True) | Q(action_type="user_block"),
                name="ck_moderation_expiry",
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["admin_user"], name="idx_mod_action_admin"),
            models.Index(fields=["target_user", "action_type"], name="idx_mod_action_target"),
            models.Index(fields=["event"], name="idx_mod_action_event"),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.action_type} by Admin {self.admin_user_id}"

    def clean(self):
        super().clean()
        if self.action_type in self.USER_ACTION_TYPES and self.target_user is None:
            raise ValidationError({"target_user": f"target_user is required for '{self.action_type}'."})
        if self.action_type in self.EVENT_ACTION_TYPES and self.event is None:
            raise ValidationError({"event": f"event is required for '{self.action_type}'."})
        if self.action_type in self.FEEDBACK_ACTION_TYPES and self.feedback is None:
            raise ValidationError({"feedback": f"feedback is required for '{self.action_type}'."})
        if self.expires_at is not None and self.action_type != "user_block":
            raise ValidationError({"expires_at": "expires_at may only be set for 'user_block' action."})


class AuditLog(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    actor_user = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="audit_logs",
    )
    action = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    entity_type = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    entity_id = models.BigIntegerField(null=True, blank=True)

    before_data = models.JSONField(null=True, blank=True)
    after_data = models.JSONField(null=True, blank=True)
    ip_address = models.GenericIPAddressField(null=True, blank=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "audit_logs"
        verbose_name = "Audit Log"
        verbose_name_plural = "Audit Logs"
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["entity_type", "entity_id"], name="idx_audit_entity"),
            models.Index(fields=["actor_user"], name="idx_audit_actor"),
            models.Index(fields=["created_at"], name="idx_audit_created"),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.action} on {self.entity_type} #{self.entity_id}"

    def save(self, *args, **kwargs):
        if self.pk is not None and not getattr(self, "_state", None).adding:
            raise ValidationError("AuditLog records are append-only and cannot be updated.")
        super().save(*args, **kwargs)

    def delete(self, *args, **kwargs):
        raise ValidationError("AuditLog records are append-only and cannot be deleted.")

# apps/audit/serializers.py
from rest_framework import serializers

from apps.audit.models import AuditLog, ModerationAction


class ModerationActionSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    admin_name = serializers.CharField(source="admin_user.display_name", read_only=True)
    target_user_name = serializers.CharField(source="target_user.display_name", read_only=True)
    event_title = serializers.CharField(source="event.title", read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = ModerationAction
        fields = [
            "id",
            "admin_user",
            "admin_name",
            "target_user",
            "target_user_name",
            "event",
            "event_title",
            "feedback",
            "action_type",
            "reason",
            "expires_at",
            "created_at",
        ]
        read_only_fields = fields


class AuditLogSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    actor_name = serializers.CharField(source="actor_user.display_name", read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = AuditLog
        fields = [
            "id",
            "actor_user",
            "actor_name",
            "action",
            "entity_type",
            "entity_id",
            "before_data",
            "after_data",
            "ip_address",
            "created_at",
        ]
        read_only_fields = fields


class ModerationReasonSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    reason = serializers.CharField(required=True)


class BlockUserSerializer(serializers.Serializer):
    reason = serializers.CharField(required=True)
    expires_at = serializers.DateTimeField(required=False, allow_null=True, default=None)

# apps/audit/services.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import CoordinatorProfile, User, UserVerificationImage
from apps.audit.models import AuditLog, ModerationAction
from apps.common.permissions import ensure_admin
from apps.events.models import Event
from apps.ops.models import Feedback


def log_action(
    actor=None,
    action: str = "",
    entity_type: str = "",
    entity_id=None,
    before=None,
    after=None,
    before_data=None,
    after_data=None,
    ip=None,
    request=None,
) -> AuditLog:
    ip_addr = ip
    if not ip_addr and request:
        ip_addr = request.META.get("REMOTE_ADDR")

    actor_user = actor if (actor and getattr(actor, "is_authenticated", False)) else None

    b_data = before if before is not None else before_data
    a_data = after if after is not None else after_data

    return AuditLog.objects.create(
        actor_user=actor_user,
        action=action,
        entity_type=entity_type,
        entity_id=entity_id,
        before_data=b_data,
        after_data=a_data,
        ip_address=ip_addr,
    )


def warn_user(admin, target_user: User, reason: str) -> ModerationAction:
    ensure_admin(admin)

    with transaction.atomic():
        action = ModerationAction.objects.create(
            admin_user=admin,
            target_user=target_user,
            action_type="user_warning",
            reason=reason,
        )

        log_action(
            actor=admin,
            action="warn_user",
            entity_type="user",
            entity_id=target_user.id,
            after_data={"reason": reason, "action_id": action.id},
        )

    return action


def block_user(admin, target_user: User, reason: str, expires_at=None) -> ModerationAction:
    ensure_admin(admin)

    with transaction.atomic():
        before = {
            "account_status": target_user.account_status,
            "blocked_until": str(target_user.blocked_until),
        }

        target_user.account_status = "blocked"
        target_user.blocked_until = expires_at
        target_user.save(update_fields=["account_status", "blocked_until"])

        action = ModerationAction.objects.create(
            admin_user=admin,
            target_user=target_user,
            action_type="user_block",
            reason=reason,
            expires_at=expires_at,
        )

        log_action(
            actor=admin,
            action="block_user",
            entity_type="user",
            entity_id=target_user.id,
            before_data=before,
            after_data={
                "account_status": "blocked",
                "blocked_until": str(expires_at),
                "action_id": action.id,
            },
        )

    return action


def unblock_user(admin, target_user: User, reason: str) -> ModerationAction:
    ensure_admin(admin)

    with transaction.atomic():
        before = {
            "account_status": target_user.account_status,
            "blocked_until": str(target_user.blocked_until),
        }

        target_user.account_status = "active"
        target_user.blocked_until = None
        target_user.save(update_fields=["account_status", "blocked_until"])

        action = ModerationAction.objects.create(
            admin_user=admin,
            target_user=target_user,
            action_type="user_unblock",
            reason=reason,
        )

        log_action(
            actor=admin,
            action="unblock_user",
            entity_type="user",
            entity_id=target_user.id,
            before_data=before,
            after_data={
                "account_status": "active",
                "blocked_until": None,
                "action_id": action.id,
            },
        )

    return action


def close_account(admin, target_user: User, reason: str) -> ModerationAction:
    ensure_admin(admin)

    with transaction.atomic():
        before = {"account_status": target_user.account_status}

        target_user.account_status = "closed"
        target_user.save(update_fields=["account_status"])

        action = ModerationAction.objects.create(
            admin_user=admin,
            target_user=target_user,
            action_type="account_close",
            reason=reason,
        )

        log_action(
            actor=admin,
            action="close_account",
            entity_type="user",
            entity_id=target_user.id,
            before_data=before,
            after_data={"account_status": "closed", "action_id": action.id},
        )

    return action


def reinstate_account(admin, target_user: User, reason: str) -> ModerationAction:
    ensure_admin(admin)

    with transaction.atomic():
        before = {"account_status": target_user.account_status}

        target_user.account_status = "active"
        target_user.save(update_fields=["account_status"])

        action = ModerationAction.objects.create(
            admin_user=admin,
            target_user=target_user,
            action_type="account_reinstate",
            reason=reason,
        )

        log_action(
            actor=admin,
            action="reinstate_account",
            entity_type="user",
            entity_id=target_user.id,
            before_data=before,
            after_data={"account_status": "active", "action_id": action.id},
        )

    return action


def delete_event(admin, event: Event, reason: str) -> ModerationAction:
    ensure_admin(admin)

    with transaction.atomic():
        now = timezone.now()
        event.deleted_at = now
        event.deleted_by = admin
        event.save(update_fields=["deleted_at", "deleted_by"])

        action = ModerationAction.objects.create(
            admin_user=admin,
            event=event,
            action_type="event_delete",
            reason=reason,
        )

        log_action(
            actor=admin,
            action="delete_event",
            entity_type="event",
            entity_id=event.id,
            after_data={"deleted_at": str(now), "action_id": action.id},
        )

    return action


def restore_event(admin, event: Event, reason: str) -> ModerationAction:
    ensure_admin(admin)

    with transaction.atomic():
        event.deleted_at = None
        event.deleted_by = None
        event.save(update_fields=["deleted_at", "deleted_by"])

        action = ModerationAction.objects.create(
            admin_user=admin,
            event=event,
            action_type="event_restore",
            reason=reason,
        )

        log_action(
            actor=admin,
            action="restore_event",
            entity_type="event",
            entity_id=event.id,
            after_data={"restored": True, "action_id": action.id},
        )

    return action


def cancel_event_by_admin(admin, event: Event, reason: str) -> ModerationAction:
    ensure_admin(admin)

    from apps.events.services import cancel_event

    with transaction.atomic():
        cancel_event(actor=admin, event=event, reason=reason)

        action = ModerationAction.objects.create(
            admin_user=admin,
            event=event,
            action_type="event_cancel",
            reason=reason,
        )

        log_action(
            actor=admin,
            action="cancel_event_by_admin",
            entity_type="event",
            entity_id=event.id,
            after_data={"reason": reason, "action_id": action.id},
        )

    return action


def hide_feedback(admin, feedback: Feedback, reason: str) -> ModerationAction:
    ensure_admin(admin)

    with transaction.atomic():
        feedback.status = "hidden"
        feedback.save(update_fields=["status"])

        action = ModerationAction.objects.create(
            admin_user=admin,
            feedback=feedback,
            action_type="feedback_hide",
            reason=reason,
        )

        log_action(
            actor=admin,
            action="hide_feedback",
            entity_type="feedback",
            entity_id=feedback.id,
            after_data={"status": "hidden", "action_id": action.id},
        )

    return action


def dashboard_stats() -> dict:
    thirty_days_ago = timezone.now() - timedelta(days=30)
    return {
        "active_users": User.objects.filter(account_status="active").count(),
        "blocked_users": User.objects.filter(account_status="blocked").count(),
        "users_pending_verification": User.objects.filter(verification_status="pending").count(),
        "images_pending_review": UserVerificationImage.objects.filter(status="pending", is_current=True).count(),
        "coordinators_pending_approval": CoordinatorProfile.objects.filter(status="pending").count(),
        "coordinators_approved": CoordinatorProfile.objects.filter(status="approved").count(),
        "events_draft": Event.objects.filter(deleted_at__isnull=True, status="draft").count(),
        "events_published": Event.objects.filter(deleted_at__isnull=True, status="published").count(),
        "events_active": Event.objects.filter(deleted_at__isnull=True, status="active").count(),
        "events_completed": Event.objects.filter(deleted_at__isnull=True, status="completed").count(),
        "events_cancelled": Event.objects.filter(deleted_at__isnull=True, status="cancelled").count(),
        "events_deleted": Event.objects.filter(deleted_at__isnull=False).count(),
        "warnings_last_30d": ModerationAction.objects.filter(
            action_type="user_warning",
            created_at__gte=thirty_days_ago,
        ).count(),
    }

# apps/audit/views.py
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.permissions import IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.accounts.models import User
from apps.audit.serializers import (
    BlockUserSerializer,
    ModerationActionSerializer,
    ModerationReasonSerializer,
)
from apps.audit.services import (
    block_user,
    cancel_event_by_admin,
    close_account,
    dashboard_stats,
    delete_event,
    hide_feedback,
    reinstate_account,
    restore_event,
    unblock_user,
    warn_user,
)
from apps.common.permissions import IsAdminRole
from apps.events.models import Event
from apps.ops.models import Feedback


class AdminDashboardStatsView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsAdminRole]

    def get(self, request):
        stats = dashboard_stats()
        return Response(stats, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class AdminWarnUserView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsAdminRole]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        target_user = get_object_or_404(User, pk=pk)
        serializer = ModerationReasonSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        action = warn_user(
            admin=request.user,
            target_user=target_user,
            reason=serializer.validated_data["reason"],
        )
        return Response(ModerationActionSerializer(action).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class AdminBlockUserView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsAdminRole]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        target_user = get_object_or_404(User, pk=pk)
        serializer = BlockUserSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        action = block_user(
            admin=request.user,
            target_user=target_user,
            reason=serializer.validated_data["reason"],
            expires_at=serializer.validated_data.get("expires_at"),
        )
        return Response(ModerationActionSerializer(action).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class AdminUnblockUserView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated, IsAdminRole]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        target_user = get_object_or_404(User, pk=pk)
        serializer = ModerationReasonSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        action = unblock_user(
            admin=request.user,
            target_user=target_user,
            reason=serializer.validated_data["reason"],
        )
        return Response(ModerationActionSerializer(action).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

# apps/common/exceptions.py
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.response import Response


class DomainError(Exception):
    code = "domain_error"
    message = "A domain error occurred."
    http_status = status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST

    def __init__(self, message=None, code=None, http_status=None):
        self.message = message or self.message
        self.code = code or self.code
        self.http_status = http_status or self.http_status
        super().__init__(self.message)


class PermissionDeniedError(DomainError):
    code = "permission_denied"
    message = "You do not have permission to perform this action."
    http_status = status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN


class AccountBlockedError(DomainError):
    code = "account_blocked"
    message = "This account is blocked."
    http_status = status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN


class AccountClosedError(DomainError):
    code = "account_closed"
    message = "This account is closed."
    http_status = status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN


class InvalidStateError(DomainError):
    code = "invalid_state"
    message = "This action is not allowed in the current state."
    http_status = status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT


class CapacityFullError(DomainError):
    code = "capacity_full"
    message = "No remaining capacity."
    http_status = status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT


class OnboardingRequiredError(DomainError):
    code = "onboarding_required"
    message = "Onboarding must be acknowledged before this action."
    http_status = status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT


class ApplicationNotAllowedError(DomainError):
    code = "application_not_allowed"
    message = "Application is not allowed."
    http_status = status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST


class QrTokenInvalidError(DomainError):
    code = "qr_token_invalid"
    message = "QR token is invalid."
    http_status = status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST


class NotFoundError(DomainError):
    code = "not_found"
    message = "Resource not found."
    http_status = status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND


class InvalidOtpError(DomainError):
    code = "invalid_otp"
    message = "Invalid OTP."
    http_status = status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST


class TooManyAttemptsError(DomainError):
    code = "too_many_attempts"
    message = "Too many attempts."
    http_status = status.HTTP_429_TOO_MANY_REQUESTS


class VerificationRequiredError(DomainError):
    code = "verification_required"
    message = "Identity verification is required."
    http_status = status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT


def domain_exception_handler(exc, context):
    if isinstance(exc, DomainError):
        return Response(
            {
                "error": {
                    "code": exc.code,
                    "message": exc.message,
                }
            },
            status=exc.http_status,
        )

    from rest_framework.views import exception_handler
    return exception_handler(exc, context)

# apps/common/fields.py
import json
from django.contrib.postgres.fields import ArrayField


class ChoiceArrayField(ArrayField):
    """
    Dual-backend ArrayField:
    - On PostgreSQL: Uses native PostgreSQL arrays (text[], varchar[]), fully compatible with PostgreSQL schema.
    - On SQLite (fallback/sandboxed tests): Stores as serialized JSON text.
    """
    def __init__(self, base_field, **kwargs):
        kwargs.setdefault("default", list)
        kwargs.setdefault("blank", True)
        super().__init__(base_field, **kwargs)

    def db_type(self, connection):
        if connection.vendor != "postgresql":
            return "text"
        return super().db_type(connection)

    def get_db_prep_value(self, value, connection, prepared=False):
        if connection.vendor != "postgresql":
            if value is None:
                return value
            return json.dumps(value)
        return super().get_db_prep_value(value, connection, prepared=prepared)

    def to_python(self, value):
        if isinstance(value, str):
            try:
                parsed = json.loads(value)
                if isinstance(parsed, list):
                    return parsed
            except Exception:
                pass
        return super().to_python(value)

    def from_db_value(self, value, expression, connection):
        if connection.vendor != "postgresql":
            if value is None:
                return value
            if isinstance(value, str):
                try:
                    return json.loads(value)
                except Exception:
                    return []
            return value
        return value

    def get_db_converters(self, connection):
        converters = super().get_db_converters(connection)
        if connection.vendor != "postgresql":
            converters.append(self.from_db_value)
        return converters

# apps/common/permissions.py
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework.permissions import BasePermission

from apps.common.constants import (
    ACCOUNT_BLOCKED,
    ACCOUNT_CLOSED,
    COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
)
from apps.common.exceptions import (
    AccountBlockedError,
    AccountClosedError,
    PermissionDeniedError,
)


def is_admin(user) -> bool:
    return bool(user and user.is_authenticated and (getattr(user, "is_admin", False) or getattr(user, "is_superuser", False)))


def is_approved_coordinator(user) -> bool:
    profile = getattr(user, "coordinator_profile", None)
    return bool(profile and getattr(profile, "status", None) == COORDINATOR_APPROVED)


def is_event_manager(user, event) -> bool:
    if not (user and user.is_authenticated):
        return False
    if is_admin(user):
        return True
    coordinator_id = getattr(event, "coordinator_id", None)
    if coordinator_id is None and hasattr(event, "coordinator"):
        coordinator_id = getattr(event.coordinator, "user_id", getattr(event.coordinator, "id", None))
    return bool(coordinator_id == getattr(user, "id", None))


def ensure_active_account(user):
    if not user or not user.is_authenticated:
        raise PermissionDeniedError("Authentication is required.")

    if getattr(user, "account_status", None) == ACCOUNT_CLOSED:
        raise AccountClosedError()

    if getattr(user, "account_status", None) == ACCOUNT_BLOCKED:
        blocked_until = getattr(user, "blocked_until", None)
        if blocked_until is None or blocked_until > timezone.now():
            raise AccountBlockedError()
        # Expired block is treated as unblocked for request purposes.

    return user


def ensure_admin(user):
    ensure_active_account(user)
    if not is_admin(user):
        raise PermissionDeniedError("Admin permission is required.")
    return user


def ensure_approved_coordinator(user):
    ensure_active_account(user)
    if not is_approved_coordinator(user):
        raise PermissionDeniedError("Approved coordinator permission is required.")
    return user


def ensure_event_manager(user, event):
    ensure_active_account(user)
    if not is_event_manager(user, event):
        raise PermissionDeniedError("You are not a manager of this event.")
    return user


class IsActiveAccount(BasePermission):
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        try:
            ensure_active_account(request.user)
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False


class IsAdminRole(BasePermission):
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        try:
            ensure_admin(request.user)
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False


class IsApprovedCoordinatorRole(BasePermission):
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        try:
            ensure_approved_coordinator(request.user)
            return True
        except Exception:
            return False

# apps/common/security.py
import base64
import hashlib
import hmac
import re
import secrets

from cryptography.fernet import Fernet
from django.conf import settings


def _secret() -> bytes:
    secret = getattr(settings, "PHONE_HASH_HMAC_SECRET", None)
    if not secret:
        raise RuntimeError("PHONE_HASH_HMAC_SECRET is not configured.")
    return str(secret).encode("utf-8")


def normalize_phone(raw_phone: str) -> str:
    """
    Minimal phone normalization.
    Keep leading + if present, otherwise digits only.
    """
    raw_phone = (raw_phone or "").strip()
    if raw_phone.startswith("+"):
        digits = re.sub(r"[^\d]", "", raw_phone[1:])
        return f"+{digits}"
    digits = re.sub(r"[^\d]", "", raw_phone)
    return digits


def compute_phone_hash(raw_phone: str) -> str:
    normalized = normalize_phone(raw_phone)
    return hmac.new(
        _secret(),
        normalized.encode("utf-8"),
        hashlib.sha256,
    ).hexdigest()


def _fernet() -> Fernet:
    key_material = hashlib.sha256(_secret()).digest()
    key = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(key_material)
    return Fernet(key)


def encrypt_phone(raw_phone: str) -> str:
    normalized = normalize_phone(raw_phone)
    return _fernet().encrypt(normalized.encode("utf-8")).decode("utf-8")


def decrypt_phone(encrypted_phone: str) -> str:
    return _fernet().decrypt(encrypted_phone.encode("utf-8")).decode("utf-8")


def generate_otp_code() -> str:
    return f"{secrets.randbelow(1_000_000):06d}"


def hash_otp(raw_code: str) -> str:
    return hmac.new(
        _secret(),
        str(raw_code).encode("utf-8"),
        hashlib.sha256,
    ).hexdigest()


def generate_raw_token() -> str:
    return secrets.token_urlsafe(32)


def hash_token(raw_token: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(raw_token.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

# apps/common/validators.py
import hashlib
import os
import uuid
from io import BytesIO
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image
from django.conf import settings

from apps.common.exceptions import DomainError


def validate_and_transcode_jpeg(uploaded_file, destination_dir: str | Path) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """
    Open image using Pillow, verify, strip EXIF, convert to RGB,
    save to destination_dir as JPEG, return (relative_file_path, sha256_hex).
    """
    try:
        img = Image.open(uploaded_file)
        # Ensure image is loaded
        img.load()
        if img.mode in ("RGBA", "LA"):
            background = Image.new("RGB", img.size, (255, 255, 255))
            background.paste(img, mask=img.split()[-1])
            rgb_image = background
        else:
            rgb_image = img.convert("RGB")
    except Exception as e:
        raise DomainError(f"Invalid or corrupted image: {str(e)}", code="invalid_image")

    dest_path = Path(destination_dir)
    dest_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    filename = f"{uuid.uuid4().hex}.jpg"
    full_path = dest_path / filename

    # Save to buffer to strip EXIF and get SHA-256
    buffer = BytesIO()
    rgb_image.save(buffer, format="JPEG", quality=90, optimize=True)
    image_bytes = buffer.getvalue()

    sha256_hex = hashlib.sha256(image_bytes).hexdigest()

    with open(full_path, "wb") as f:
        f.write(image_bytes)

    # Determine relative path
    media_root = getattr(settings, "MEDIA_ROOT", None)
    if media_root:
        try:
            rel_path = full_path.relative_to(Path(media_root)).as_posix()
        except ValueError:
            rel_path = f"{dest_path.name}/{filename}"
    else:
        rel_path = f"{dest_path.name}/{filename}"

    return rel_path, sha256_hex

# apps/events/admin.py
from django.contrib import admin

from apps.audit.services import delete_event, restore_event
from apps.events.models import (
    CharityField,
    Event,
    EventApplication,
    EventCharityField,
    EventPost,
)
from apps.events.services import activate_event, publish_event


@admin.register(CharityField)
class CharityFieldAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = ("code", "label", "active", "sort_order")
    list_editable = ("active", "sort_order")
    search_fields = ("code", "label")


class EventCharityFieldInline(admin.TabularInline):
    model = EventCharityField
    extra = 1


class EventPostInline(admin.StackedInline):
    model = EventPost
    extra = 1


@admin.register(Event)
class EventAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = (
        "id",
        "title",
        "coordinator",
        "purpose_type",
        "status",
        "start_at",
        "end_at",
        "deleted_at",
    )
    list_filter = (
        "status",
        "purpose_type",
        "compensation_kind",
        "meals_provided",
        "lodging_provided",
    )
    search_fields = ("title", "description", "location_name")
    inlines = [EventCharityFieldInline, EventPostInline]
    actions = [
        "publish_event",
        "activate_event",
        "soft_delete_event",
        "restore_event",
        "publish_selected_events",
        "activate_selected_events",
        "soft_delete_selected_events",
        "restore_selected_events",
    ]

    @admin.action(description="Publish selected draft events")
    def publish_event(self, request, queryset):
        for event in queryset:
            if event.status == "draft":
                try:
                    publish_event(actor=request.user, event=event)
                except Exception as e:
                    self.message_user(request, f"Error publishing event #{event.id}: {e}", level="error")

    @admin.action(description="Activate selected published events")
    def activate_event(self, request, queryset):
        for event in queryset:
            if event.status == "published":
                try:
                    activate_event(actor=request.user, event=event)
                except Exception as e:
                    self.message_user(request, f"Error activating event #{event.id}: {e}", level="error")

    @admin.action(description="Soft delete selected events")
    def soft_delete_event(self, request, queryset):
        for event in queryset:
            if event.deleted_at is None:
                try:
                    delete_event(admin=request.user, event=event, reason="Deleted via admin panel")
                except Exception as e:
                    self.message_user(request, f"Error deleting event #{event.id}: {e}", level="error")

    @admin.action(description="Restore selected deleted events")
    def restore_event(self, request, queryset):
        for event in queryset:
            if event.deleted_at is not None:
                try:
                    restore_event(admin=request.user, event=event, reason="Restored via admin panel")
                except Exception as e:
                    self.message_user(request, f"Error restoring event #{event.id}: {e}", level="error")

    publish_selected_events = publish_event
    activate_selected_events = activate_event
    soft_delete_selected_events = soft_delete_event
    restore_selected_events = restore_event


@admin.register(EventPost)
class EventPostAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = (
        "id",
        "title",
        "event",
        "required_headcount",
        "physical_level",
        "status",
        "priority",
    )
    list_filter = ("status", "physical_level", "leader_needed")
    search_fields = ("title", "event__title")


@admin.register(EventApplication)
class EventApplicationAdmin(admin.ModelAdmin):
    list_display = (
        "id",
        "event",
        "user",
        "post",
        "source",
        "status",
        "applied_at",
        "decided_at",
    )
    list_filter = ("status", "source")
    search_fields = ("user__display_name", "event__title")
    readonly_fields = ("applied_at", "decided_at", "decided_by", "conditions_snapshot")

# apps/events/models.py
from django.contrib.postgres.indexes import GinIndex
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import models
from django.db.models import F, Q

from apps.common.constants import (
    APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
    APPLICATION_EXPIRED,
    APPLICATION_PENDING,
    APPLICATION_REJECTED,
    APPLICATION_WAITLISTED,
    APPLICATION_WITHDRAWN,
    EVENT_ACTIVE,
    EVENT_CANCELLED,
    EVENT_COMPLETED,
    EVENT_DRAFT,
    EVENT_PUBLISHED,
)
from apps.common.fields import ChoiceArrayField


class CharityField(models.Model):
    code = models.CharField(max_length=50, primary_key=True)
    label = models.CharField(max_length=100)
    active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    sort_order = models.IntegerField(default=100)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "charity_fields"
        ordering = ["sort_order", "code"]
        verbose_name = "Charity Field"
        verbose_name_plural = "Charity Fields"

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.label} ({self.code})"


class EventQuerySet(models.QuerySet):
    def visible(self):
        return self.filter(deleted_at__isnull=True)


class Event(models.Model):
    PURPOSE_TYPE_CHOICES = [
        ("charity_event", "Charity Event"),
        ("help_wanted_event", "Help Wanted Event"),
    ]

    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        (EVENT_DRAFT, "Draft"),
        (EVENT_PUBLISHED, "Published"),
        (EVENT_ACTIVE, "Active"),
        (EVENT_COMPLETED, "Completed"),
        (EVENT_CANCELLED, "Cancelled"),
    ]

    ACCEPTANCE_MODE_CHOICES = [
        ("auto", "Auto"),
        ("manual", "Manual"),
    ]

    LEADER_STRATEGY_CHOICES = [
        ("none", "None"),
        ("first_experienced", "First Experienced"),
        ("manual", "Manual"),
    ]

    COMPENSATION_KIND_CHOICES = [
        ("unpaid", "Unpaid"),
        ("arranged_outside_system", "Arranged Outside System"),
    ]

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)

    coordinator = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.PROTECT,
        related_name="coordinated_events",
    )

    title = models.CharField(max_length=255)
    description = models.TextField(null=True, blank=True)
    purpose_type = models.CharField(
        max_length=30,
        choices=PURPOSE_TYPE_CHOICES,
        default="charity_event",
    )

    location_name = models.CharField(max_length=255, null=True, blank=True)
    landmark_text = models.TextField(null=True, blank=True)

    start_at = models.DateTimeField(db_index=True)
    end_at = models.DateTimeField()
    application_deadline = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=STATUS_CHOICES,
        default=EVENT_DRAFT,
    )

    acceptance_mode = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=ACCEPTANCE_MODE_CHOICES,
        default="manual",
    )
    auto_assign_enabled = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    group_size = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
    leader_strategy = models.CharField(
        max_length=30,
        choices=LEADER_STRATEGY_CHOICES,
        default="none",
    )
    max_participants = models.IntegerField(null=True, blank=True)

    # Recruiting conditions (informational only)
    meals_provided = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    lodging_provided = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    travel_support_provided = models.BooleanField(default=False)

    compensation_kind = models.CharField(
        max_length=30,
        choices=COMPENSATION_KIND_CHOICES,
        default="unpaid",
    )
    compensation_note = models.TextField(null=True, blank=True)

    # Onboarding
    onboarding_text = models.TextField(null=True, blank=True)
    onboarding_version = models.IntegerField(default=1)
    require_onboarding_ack = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    # Walk-in QR registration
    qr_registration_enabled = models.BooleanField(default=True)

    # Soft delete
    deleted_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    deleted_by = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="deleted_events",
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    objects = EventQuerySet.as_manager()

    class Meta:
        db_table = "events"
        verbose_name = "Event"
        verbose_name_plural = "Events"
        constraints = [
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(end_at__gt=F("start_at")),
                name="ck_events_time",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(application_deadline__isnull=True)
                | Q(application_deadline__lte=F("start_at")),
                name="ck_events_deadline",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=(
                    Q(deleted_at__isnull=True) & Q(deleted_by__isnull=True)
                )
                | (
                    Q(deleted_at__isnull=False) & Q(deleted_by__isnull=False)
                ),
                name="ck_events_soft_delete",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(group_size__isnull=True) | Q(group_size__gt=0),
                name="ck_events_group_size",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(max_participants__isnull=True) | Q(max_participants__gt=0),
                name="ck_events_max_participants",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(onboarding_version__gte=1),
                name="ck_events_onboarding_version",
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["coordinator"], name="idx_events_coordinator"),
            models.Index(fields=["start_at"], name="idx_events_start_at"),
            models.Index(
                fields=["status", "start_at"],
                name="idx_events_visible",
                condition=Q(deleted_at__isnull=True),
            ),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.title} ({self.status})"

    def clean(self):
        super().clean()
        if self.start_at and self.end_at and self.end_at <= self.start_at:
            raise ValidationError({"end_at": "end_at must be greater than start_at."})
        if self.application_deadline and self.start_at and self.application_deadline > self.start_at:
            raise ValidationError({"application_deadline": "application_deadline must be <= start_at."})
        if (self.deleted_at is None) != (self.deleted_by is None):
            raise ValidationError("deleted_at and deleted_by must be set together or both null.")
        if self.group_size is not None and self.group_size <= 0:
            raise ValidationError({"group_size": "group_size must be greater than 0."})
        if self.max_participants is not None and self.max_participants <= 0:
            raise ValidationError({"max_participants": "max_participants must be greater than 0."})
        if self.onboarding_version is not None and self.onboarding_version < 1:
            raise ValidationError({"onboarding_version": "onboarding_version must be >= 1."})


class EventCharityField(models.Model):
    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    event = models.ForeignKey(
        Event,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="charity_links",
    )
    field_code = models.ForeignKey(
        CharityField,
        on_delete=models.PROTECT,
        db_column="field_code",
        related_name="event_links",
    )

    class Meta:
        db_table = "event_charity_fields"
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["event", "field_code"],
                name="uq_event_charity_field",
            ),
        ]
        verbose_name = "Event Charity Field"
        verbose_name_plural = "Event Charity Fields"

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.event.title} -> {self.field_code.label}"


class EventPost(models.Model):
    PHYSICAL_LEVEL_CHOICES = [
        ("none", "None"),
        ("light", "Light"),
        ("medium", "Medium"),
        ("heavy", "Heavy"),
    ]

    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        ("open", "Open"),
        ("full", "Full"),
        ("closed", "Closed"),
    ]

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    event = models.ForeignKey(
        Event,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="posts",
    )

    title = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    description = models.TextField(null=True, blank=True)
    required_headcount = models.IntegerField()

    physical_level = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=PHYSICAL_LEVEL_CHOICES,
        default="none",
    )
    required_skills = ChoiceArrayField(
        models.CharField(max_length=50),
        default=list,
        blank=True,
    )

    leader_needed = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    new_volunteers_allowed = models.BooleanField(default=True)
    priority = models.IntegerField(default=100)

    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=STATUS_CHOICES,
        default="open",
    )

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "event_posts"
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["event", "title"],
                name="uq_event_post_event_title",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=Q(required_headcount__gt=0),
                name="ck_event_posts_headcount",
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [
            GinIndex(fields=["required_skills"], name="idx_event_posts_skills_gin"),
        ]
        verbose_name = "Event Post"
        verbose_name_plural = "Event Posts"

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.title} @ {self.event.title}"

    def clean(self):
        super().clean()
        if self.required_headcount is not None and self.required_headcount <= 0:
            raise ValidationError({"required_headcount": "required_headcount must be greater than 0."})


class EventApplication(models.Model):
    SOURCE_CHOICES = [
        ("browse", "Browse"),
        ("qr_walkin", "QR Walk-in"),
        ("invite", "Invite"),
    ]

    STATUS_CHOICES = [
        (APPLICATION_PENDING, "Pending"),
        (APPLICATION_ACCEPTED, "Accepted"),
        (APPLICATION_WAITLISTED, "Waitlisted"),
        (APPLICATION_REJECTED, "Rejected"),
        (APPLICATION_WITHDRAWN, "Withdrawn"),
        (APPLICATION_EXPIRED, "Expired"),
    ]

    id = models.BigAutoField(primary_key=True)
    event = models.ForeignKey(
        Event,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="applications",
    )
    post = models.ForeignKey(
        EventPost,
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="applications",
    )
    user = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.CASCADE,
        related_name="applications",
    )
    qr_token = models.ForeignKey(
        "ops.QrToken",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="applications",
    )

    source = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=SOURCE_CHOICES,
        default="browse",
    )
    status = models.CharField(
        max_length=20,
        choices=STATUS_CHOICES,
        default=APPLICATION_PENDING,
        db_index=True,
    )

    applied_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    decided_by = models.ForeignKey(
        "accounts.User",
        on_delete=models.SET_NULL,
        null=True,
        blank=True,
        related_name="decided_applications",
    )
    decided_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)

    conditions_acknowledged_at = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
    conditions_snapshot = models.JSONField(null=True, blank=True)
    note = models.TextField(null=True, blank=True)

    created_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    updated_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)

    class Meta:
        db_table = "event_applications"
        verbose_name = "Event Application"
        verbose_name_plural = "Event Applications"
        constraints = [
            models.UniqueConstraint(
                fields=["event", "user"],
                condition=Q(status__in=["pending", "accepted", "waitlisted"]),
                name="uq_event_application_active",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=~Q(source="qr_walkin") | Q(qr_token__isnull=False),
                name="ck_application_walkin_token",
            ),
            models.CheckConstraint(
                condition=~Q(status__in=["accepted", "rejected"])
                | Q(decided_at__isnull=False),
                name="ck_application_decided",
            ),
        ]
        indexes = [
            models.Index(fields=["event", "status"], name="idx_event_apps_status"),
        ]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.user.display_name} -> {self.event.title} [{self.status}]"

    def clean(self):
        super().clean()
        if self.source == "qr_walkin" and self.qr_token is None:
            raise ValidationError({"qr_token": "qr_token is required when source is 'qr_walkin'."})
        if self.status in [APPLICATION_ACCEPTED, APPLICATION_REJECTED] and self.decided_at is None:
            raise ValidationError({"decided_at": f"decided_at is required when status is '{self.status}'."})
