# apps/ops/views.py
from django.shortcuts import get_object_or_404
from rest_framework import generics, status
from rest_framework.permissions import AllowAny, IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.accounts.models import User
from apps.common.security import hash_token
from apps.events.models import Event, EventApplication, EventPost
from apps.events.serializers import EventApplicationSerializer
from apps.ops.models import Assignment, Attendance, Feedback, QrToken
from apps.ops.serializers import (
    AssignmentCancelSerializer,
    AssignmentSerializer,
    AttendanceSerializer,
    CheckInSerializer,
    CheckOutSerializer,
    FeedbackCreateSerializer,
    FeedbackSerializer,
    ManualAssignmentSerializer,
    MarkAbsentSerializer,
    OnboardingAcknowledgeSerializer,
    OnboardingAcknowledgmentSerializer,
    QrTokenGenerateSerializer,
    QrTokenRedeemSerializer,
    QrTokenResolveSerializer,
    QrTokenSerializer,
)
from apps.ops.services import (
    acknowledge_onboarding,
    assign_manually,
    cancel_assignment,
    check_in,
    check_out,
    confirm_assignment,
    generate_qr_token,
    mark_absent,
    redeem_qr_token,
    resolve_qr_token,
    run_auto_assignment,
    submit_feedback,
)


class EventQrGenerateView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        serializer = QrTokenGenerateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        qr_token, raw_token = generate_qr_token(
            actor=request.user,
            event=event,
            purpose=serializer.validated_data["purpose"],
            max_uses=serializer.validated_data.get("max_uses"),
            ttl_minutes=serializer.validated_data.get("ttl_minutes", 240),
        )

        return Response(
            {
                "raw_token": raw_token,
                "token": raw_token,
                "qr_token": QrTokenSerializer(qr_token).data,
            },
            status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        )


class QrResolveView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]

    def post(self, request):
        serializer = QrTokenResolveSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        preview = resolve_qr_token(raw_token=serializer.validated_data["token"])
        return Response(preview, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class QrRedeemView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request):
        serializer = QrTokenRedeemSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        res = redeem_qr_token(user=request.user, raw_token=serializer.validated_data["token"])
        return Response(res, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EventAutoRunAssignmentView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        assignments = run_auto_assignment(actor=request.user, event=event)
        return Response(
            AssignmentSerializer(assignments, many=True).data,
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        )


class EventManualAssignmentView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        serializer = ManualAssignmentSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        application = get_object_or_404(
            EventApplication,
            pk=serializer.validated_data["application_id"],
            event=event,
        )

        post = None
        if serializer.validated_data.get("post_id"):
            post = get_object_or_404(
                EventPost,
                pk=serializer.validated_data["post_id"],
                event=event,
            )

        assignment = assign_manually(
            actor=request.user,
            application=application,
            post=post,
            group_code=serializer.validated_data.get("group_code"),
            is_group_leader=serializer.validated_data.get("is_group_leader", False),
        )

        return Response(AssignmentSerializer(assignment).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class AssignmentConfirmView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        assignment = get_object_or_404(Assignment, pk=pk)
        updated = confirm_assignment(user=request.user, assignment=assignment)
        return Response(AssignmentSerializer(updated).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class AssignmentCancelView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        assignment = get_object_or_404(Assignment, pk=pk)
        serializer = AssignmentCancelSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        cancelled = cancel_assignment(
            actor=request.user,
            assignment=assignment,
            reason=serializer.validated_data.get("reason", ""),
        )
        return Response(AssignmentSerializer(cancelled).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EventCheckInView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        serializer = CheckInSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        target_user = request.user
        if serializer.validated_data.get("user_id"):
            target_user = get_object_or_404(User, pk=serializer.validated_data["user_id"])

        qr_token = None
        raw_token = serializer.validated_data.get("token")
        if raw_token:
            qr_token = QrToken.objects.filter(token_hash=hash_token(raw_token)).first()

        attendance = check_in(
            actor=request.user,
            event=event,
            user=target_user,
            method=serializer.validated_data.get("method", "qr_scan"),
            qr_token=qr_token,
        )
        return Response(AttendanceSerializer(attendance).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EventCheckOutView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        serializer = CheckOutSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        target_user = request.user
        if serializer.validated_data.get("user_id"):
            target_user = get_object_or_404(User, pk=serializer.validated_data["user_id"])

        attendance = check_out(actor=request.user, event=event, user=target_user)
        return Response(AttendanceSerializer(attendance).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EventMarkAbsentView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        serializer = MarkAbsentSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        target_user = get_object_or_404(User, pk=serializer.validated_data["user_id"])
        attendance = mark_absent(actor=request.user, event=event, user=target_user)
        return Response(AttendanceSerializer(attendance).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EventOnboardingAcknowledgeView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        serializer = OnboardingAcknowledgeSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        ack = acknowledge_onboarding(
            user=request.user,
            event=event,
            method=serializer.validated_data.get("method", "app_tick"),
        )
        return Response(OnboardingAcknowledgmentSerializer(ack).data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EventFeedbackView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk):
        event = get_object_or_404(Event, pk=pk)
        serializer = FeedbackCreateSerializer(data=request.data)
        serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)

        target_user = None
        if serializer.validated_data.get("target_user_id"):
            target_user = get_object_or_404(User, pk=serializer.validated_data["target_user_id"])

        feedback = submit_feedback(
            user=request.user,
            event=event,
            direction=serializer.validated_data["direction"],
            target_user=target_user,
            score=serializer.validated_data["score"],
            tags=serializer.validated_data.get("tags", []),
            comment=serializer.validated_data.get("comment", ""),
        )
        return Response(FeedbackSerializer(feedback).data, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class MeApplicationsListView(generics.ListAPIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    serializer_class = EventApplicationSerializer

    def get_queryset(self):
        if getattr(self, "swagger_fake_view", False) or not getattr(self.request.user, "is_authenticated", False):
            return EventApplication.objects.none()
        return (
            EventApplication.objects.filter(user=self.request.user)
            .select_related("event", "post")
            .order_by("-applied_at")
        )


class MeAssignmentsListView(generics.ListAPIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    serializer_class = AssignmentSerializer

    def get_queryset(self):
        if getattr(self, "swagger_fake_view", False) or not getattr(self.request.user, "is_authenticated", False):
            return Assignment.objects.none()
        return (
            Assignment.objects.filter(user=self.request.user)
            .select_related("event", "post", "user")
            .order_by("-created_at")
        )

# Generated to ensure PostgreSQL column names match Django models and contract
from django.db import migrations


def align_postgres(apps, schema_editor):
    if schema_editor.connection.vendor != "postgresql":
        return

    with schema_editor.connection.cursor() as cursor:
        # 1. users.password drop not null
        cursor.execute("ALTER TABLE users ALTER COLUMN password DROP NOT NULL;")

        # 2. event_charity_fields rename field_id to field_code if field_id exists
        cursor.execute("""
            DO $$
            BEGIN
                IF EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns 
                    WHERE table_name = 'event_charity_fields' AND column_name = 'field_id'
                ) AND NOT EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns 
                    WHERE table_name = 'event_charity_fields' AND column_name = 'field_code'
                ) THEN
                    ALTER TABLE event_charity_fields RENAME COLUMN field_id TO field_code;
                END IF;
            END $$;
        """)

        # 3. user_charity_interests rename field_id to field_code if field_id exists
        cursor.execute("""
            DO $$
            BEGIN
                IF EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns 
                    WHERE table_name = 'user_charity_interests' AND column_name = 'field_id'
                ) AND NOT EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns 
                    WHERE table_name = 'user_charity_interests' AND column_name = 'field_code'
                ) THEN
                    ALTER TABLE user_charity_interests RENAME COLUMN field_id TO field_code;
                END IF;
            END $$;
        """)


class Migration(migrations.Migration):

    dependencies = [
        ("accounts", "0002_initial"),
        ("events", "0002_initial"),
    ]

    operations = [
        migrations.RunPython(align_postgres, reverse_code=migrations.RunPython.noop),
    ]

C:\Users\USER\Documents\INTERN\Project\Volunteer HR Management\python stack\apps\events\management\commands\seed_charity_fields.py

from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand
from apps.events.models import CharityField

INITIAL_CHARITY_FIELDS = [
    ('teaching', 'Teaching', 10),
    ('food_distribution', 'Food Distribution', 20),
    ('elderly_care', 'Elderly Care', 30),
    ('child_care', 'Child Care', 40),
    ('animal_welfare', 'Animal Welfare', 50),
    ('environment', 'Environment', 60),
    ('disaster_support', 'Disaster Support', 70),
    ('blood_donation', 'Blood Donation', 80),
    ('fundraising', 'Fundraising', 90),
    ('logistics', 'Logistics', 100),
    ('cleaning', 'Cleaning', 110),
    ('cooking', 'Cooking', 120),
    ('other', 'Other', 999),
]


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = 'Seeds initial charity fields from volunteer_coordination_system_db_v0_5.sql'

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        created_count = 0
        updated_count = 0

        for code, label, sort_order in INITIAL_CHARITY_FIELDS:
            obj, created = CharityField.objects.update_or_create(
                code=code,
                defaults={'label': label, 'sort_order': sort_order, 'active': True}
            )
            if created:
                created_count += 1
            else:
                updated_count += 1

        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                f'Successfully seeded charity fields. Created: {created_count}, Updated: {updated_count}'
            )
        )

# apps/events/management/commands/seed_demo_data.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.contrib.auth.hashers import make_password
from django.core.management.base import BaseCommand
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import CoordinatorProfile, User
from apps.common.constants import (
    APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
    ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED,
    ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
    COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
    EVENT_PUBLISHED,
    VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
)
from apps.common.security import compute_phone_hash
from apps.events.models import CharityField, Event, EventApplication, EventCharityField, EventPost
from apps.ops.models import Assignment, Attendance


class Command(BaseCommand):
    help = "Seeds demo users, coordinator, event, posts, and confirmed assignment (idempotent)."

    def handle(self, *args, **options):
        now = timezone.now()

        # 1. Admin User
        admin_phone = "+959111111111"
        admin_hash = compute_phone_hash(admin_phone)
        admin_user, _ = User.objects.update_or_create(
            phone_hash=admin_hash,
            defaults={
                "display_name": "Demo Admin",
                "password": make_password("admin123"),
                "is_admin": True,
                "is_staff": True,
                "is_superuser": True,
                "account_status": "active",
                "verification_status": VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
            },
        )

        # Superadmin credentials for Django Admin panel login
        superadmin_user, _ = User.objects.update_or_create(
            phone_hash="12345",
            defaults={
                "display_name": "admin",
                "password": make_password("12345"),
                "is_admin": True,
                "is_staff": True,
                "is_superuser": True,
                "account_status": "active",
                "verification_status": VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
            },
        )

        # 2. Approved Coordinator
        coord_phone = "+959222222222"
        coord_hash = compute_phone_hash(coord_phone)
        coord_user, _ = User.objects.update_or_create(
            phone_hash=coord_hash,
            defaults={
                "phone_enc": f"ENC_{coord_phone}",
                "display_name": "Demo Coordinator",
                "password": make_password(None),
                "account_status": "active",
                "verification_status": VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
            },
        )
        CoordinatorProfile.objects.update_or_create(
            user=coord_user,
            defaults={
                "status": COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
                "approved_by": admin_user,
                "approved_at": now,
                "notes": "Demo coordinator approved profile.",
            },
        )

        # 3. Volunteers
        vol1_phone = "+959333333333"
        vol1_hash = compute_phone_hash(vol1_phone)
        vol1_user, _ = User.objects.update_or_create(
            phone_hash=vol1_hash,
            defaults={
                "phone_enc": f"ENC_{vol1_phone}",
                "display_name": "Demo Volunteer Alpha",
                "password": make_password(None),
                "account_status": "active",
                "skills": ["teaching", "logistics"],
                "has_leader_experience": True,
                "physical_ok": True,
                "can_lift_heavy": True,
                "new_to_volunteer": False,
            },
        )

        vol2_phone = "+959444444444"
        vol2_hash = compute_phone_hash(vol2_phone)
        vol2_user, _ = User.objects.update_or_create(
            phone_hash=vol2_hash,
            defaults={
                "phone_enc": f"ENC_{vol2_phone}",
                "display_name": "Demo Volunteer Beta",
                "password": make_password(None),
                "account_status": "active",
                "skills": ["cooking"],
                "has_leader_experience": False,
                "physical_ok": True,
                "can_lift_heavy": False,
                "new_to_volunteer": True,
            },
        )

        # 4. Published Event
        event, _ = Event.objects.update_or_create(
            coordinator=coord_user,
            title="Yangon Community Relief Drive",
            defaults={
                "description": "Annual food and education relief program for Yangon river communities.",
                "purpose_type": "charity_event",
                "status": EVENT_PUBLISHED,
                "location_name": "Yangon Riverside Center",
                "start_at": now + timedelta(days=5),
                "end_at": now + timedelta(days=5, hours=8),
                "application_deadline": now + timedelta(days=4),
                "acceptance_mode": "auto",
                "max_participants": 30,
                "group_size": 5,
                "require_onboarding_ack": True,
                "onboarding_text": "Please bring photo ID, water bottle, and safety footwear.",
                "onboarding_version": 1,
            },
        )

        # Link charity fields
        logistics_field = CharityField.objects.filter(code="logistics").first()
        teaching_field = CharityField.objects.filter(code="teaching").first()
        if logistics_field:
            EventCharityField.objects.get_or_create(event=event, field_code=logistics_field)
        if teaching_field:
            EventCharityField.objects.get_or_create(event=event, field_code=teaching_field)

        # 5. Three Posts with distinct criteria
        post1, _ = EventPost.objects.update_or_create(
            event=event,
            title="Logistics & Heavy Distribution",
            defaults={
                "description": "Loading trucks and carrying food supply packages.",
                "required_headcount": 8,
                "physical_level": "heavy",
                "required_skills": ["logistics"],
                "leader_needed": True,
                "status": "open",
                "priority": 1,
            },
        )

        post2, _ = EventPost.objects.update_or_create(
            event=event,
            title="Youth Workshop Facilitator",
            defaults={
                "description": "Leading community education and skills sessions.",
                "required_headcount": 6,
                "physical_level": "light",
                "required_skills": ["teaching"],
                "leader_needed": True,
                "status": "open",
                "priority": 2,
            },
        )

        post3, _ = EventPost.objects.update_or_create(
            event=event,
            title="Community Kitchen Helper",
            defaults={
                "description": "Food preparation and general helper duties.",
                "required_headcount": 10,
                "physical_level": "medium",
                "required_skills": ["cooking"],
                "new_volunteers_allowed": True,
                "status": "open",
                "priority": 3,
            },
        )

        # 6. Accepted Application & Confirmed Assignment for Volunteer 1
        application, _ = EventApplication.objects.update_or_create(
            event=event,
            user=vol1_user,
            defaults={
                "post": post1,
                "source": "browse",
                "status": APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
                "decided_by": coord_user,
                "decided_at": now,
                "conditions_snapshot": {
                    "meals_provided": event.meals_provided,
                    "lodging_provided": event.lodging_provided,
                },
            },
        )

        assignment, _ = Assignment.objects.update_or_create(
            event=event,
            user=vol1_user,
            defaults={
                "application": application,
                "post": post1,
                "status": ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED,
                "assigned_by": "coordinator",
                "assigned_by_user": coord_user,
                "group_code": "G1",
                "is_group_leader": True,
                "schedule_start": event.start_at,
                "schedule_end": event.end_at,
                "conditions_snapshot": application.conditions_snapshot or {},
            },
        )

        Attendance.objects.update_or_create(
            event=event,
            user=vol1_user,
            defaults={
                "assignment": assignment,
                "status": ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
            },
        )

        self.stdout.write(
            self.style.SUCCESS(
                f"Successfully seeded demo data: 1 admin ({admin_user.display_name}), "
                f"1 coordinator ({coord_user.display_name}), 2 volunteers, "
                f"1 event ('{event.title}') with 3 posts, 1 confirmed assignment."
            )
        )

----------------------------------------
import os
from pathlib import Path

# Target output directory
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("combined_code_v2")
OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

# Directories and patterns to skip
EXCLUDED_DIRS = {".venv", "venv", ".env", "env", "__pycache__", ".git", ".idea", ".vscode"}

# Full and half divider lines
LINE_FULL = "=" * 80
LINE_HALF = "-" * 40

def get_target_group_filename(file_path: Path, root_path: Path) -> str:
    """Determines which .txt file a .py script belongs to."""
    relative = file_path.relative_to(root_path)
    parts = relative.parts
    is_test = "test" in file_path.stem.lower() or any("test" in p.lower() for p in parts[:-1])

    if len(parts) == 1:
        # Root-level files (e.g., manage.py)
        return "tests_root.txt" if is_test else "root_config.txt"

    # Identify primary module or subfolder name
    # e.g., if app/volunteers/views.py -> group by "app_volunteers"
    if parts[0] == "app" and len(parts) > 2:
        group_name = f"app_{parts[1]}"
    else:
        group_name = parts[0]

    if is_test:
        return f"tests_{group_name}.txt"
    return f"{group_name}.txt"

def main():
    root = Path.cwd()
    merged_files = {}

    print(f"Scanning directory: {root}")

    for path in root.rglob("*.py"):
        # Skip if any parent folder is in the exclusion list
        if any(part in EXCLUDED_DIRS for part in path.parts):
            continue

        target_txt = get_target_group_filename(path, root)
        output_file_path = OUTPUT_DIR / target_txt

        try:
            content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Could not read {path}: {e}")
            continue

        with open(output_file_path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as out:
            out.write(f"{LINE_FULL}\n")
            out.write(f"{path.resolve()}\n")
            out.write(f"{LINE_HALF}\n")
            out.write(content.strip() + "\n\n")

        merged_files[target_txt] = merged_files.get(target_txt, 0) + 1

    print("\nExtraction complete! Files generated in 'combined_code_v2/':")
    for fname, count in merged_files.items():
        print(f"  - {fname}: {count} file(s) merged")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Django's command-line utility for administrative tasks."""
import os
import sys


def main():
    """Run administrative tasks."""
    os.environ.setdefault('DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE', 'backend.settings')
    try:

    except ImportError as exc:
        raise ImportError(
            "Couldn't import Django. Are you sure it's installed and "
            "available on your PYTHONPATH environment variable? Did you "
            "forget to activate a virtual environment?"
        ) from exc
    execute_from_command_line(sys.argv)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

C:\Users\USER\Documents\INTERN\Project\Volunteer HR Management\python stack\concat_code.py
----------------------------------------
import os
from pathlib import Path

# Target output directory
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("combined_code_v2")
OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

# Directories and patterns to skip
EXCLUDED_DIRS = {".venv", "venv", ".env", "env", "__pycache__", ".git", ".idea", ".vscode"}

# Full and half divider lines
LINE_FULL = "=" * 80
LINE_HALF = "-" * 40

def get_target_group_filename(file_path: Path, root_path: Path) -> str:
    """Determines which .txt file a .py script belongs to."""
    relative = file_path.relative_to(root_path)
    parts = relative.parts
    is_test = "test" in file_path.stem.lower() or any("test" in p.lower() for p in parts[:-1])

    if len(parts) == 1:
        # Root-level files (e.g., manage.py)
        return "tests_root.txt" if is_test else "root_config.txt"

    # Identify primary module or subfolder name
    # e.g., if app/volunteers/views.py -> group by "app_volunteers"
    if parts[0] == "app" and len(parts) > 2:
        group_name = f"app_{parts[1]}"
    else:
        group_name = parts[0]

    if is_test:
        return f"tests_{group_name}.txt"
    return f"{group_name}.txt"

def main():
    root = Path.cwd()
    merged_files = {}

    print(f"Scanning directory: {root}")

    for path in root.rglob("*.py"):
        # Skip if any parent folder is in the exclusion list
        if any(part in EXCLUDED_DIRS for part in path.parts):
            continue

        target_txt = get_target_group_filename(path, root)
        output_file_path = OUTPUT_DIR / target_txt

        try:
            content = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Could not read {path}: {e}")
            continue

        with open(output_file_path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as out:
            out.write(f"{LINE_FULL}\n")
            out.write(f"{path.resolve()}\n")
            out.write(f"{LINE_HALF}\n")
            out.write(content.strip() + "\n\n")

        merged_files[target_txt] = merged_files.get(target_txt, 0) + 1

    print("\nExtraction complete! Files generated in 'combined_code_v2/':")
    for fname, count in merged_files.items():
        print(f"  - {fname}: {count} file(s) merged")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python
"""Django's command-line utility for administrative tasks."""
import os
import sys


def main():
    """Run administrative tasks."""
    os.environ.setdefault('DJANGO_SETTINGS_MODULE', 'backend.settings')
    try:
        from django.core.management import execute_from_command_line
    except ImportError as exc:
        raise ImportError(
            "Couldn't import Django. Are you sure it's installed and "
            "available on your PYTHONPATH environment variable? Did you "
            "forget to activate a virtual environment?"
        ) from exc
    execute_from_command_line(sys.argv)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

# apps/accounts/tests/test_api.py
from io import BytesIO
from PIL import Image
from django.core.files.uploadedfile import SimpleUploadedFile
from django.urls import reverse
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.test import APITestCase
from rest_framework.authtoken.models import Token

from apps.accounts.models import CoordinatorProfile, User, UserVerificationImage
from apps.accounts.services import issue_token_for_user
from apps.common.constants import (
    COORDINATOR_PENDING,
    IMAGE_MOCK_SELFIE,
    VERIFICATION_NONE,
)
from apps.events.models import CharityField


def create_dummy_image_file(filename="selfie.png"):
    buffer = BytesIO()
    image = Image.new("RGBA", (100, 100), color="blue")
    image.save(buffer, format="PNG")
    buffer.seek(0)
    return SimpleUploadedFile(filename, buffer.getvalue(), content_type="image/png")


class AccountsApiTests(APITestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.phone = "+959987654321"

    def test_otp_flow_api_returns_token(self):
        # 1. Request OTP
        req_resp = self.client.post("/api/v1/auth/otp/request/", {"phone": self.phone})
        self.assertEqual(req_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(req_resp.data.get("status"), "sent")
        code = req_resp.data.get("dev_code")
        self.assertIsNotNone(code)

        # 2. Verify OTP
        verify_resp = self.client.post("/api/v1/auth/otp/verify/", {"phone": self.phone, "code": code})
        self.assertEqual(verify_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertIn("token", verify_resp.data)
        self.assertIn("user", verify_resp.data)
        self.assertTrue(verify_resp.data.get("created"))
        self.assertEqual(verify_resp.data["user"]["display_name"], f"Volunteer {self.phone[-4:]}")

    def test_me_patch_cannot_elevate_admin(self):
        # Create user & token
        user = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_user_patch",
            display_name="Regular Volunteer",
            is_admin=False,
        )
        token = issue_token_for_user(user)
        self.client.credentials(HTTP_AUTHORIZATION=f"Token {token.key}")

        # Attempt to patch regular fields - should succeed
        patch_resp = self.client.patch("/api/v1/me/", {"display_name": "Updated Name", "home_area": "Yangon"}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(patch_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(patch_resp.data["display_name"], "Updated Name")
        self.assertEqual(patch_resp.data["home_area"], "Yangon")

        # Attempt to escalate to admin - MUST fail with 400 Bad Request
        elevate_resp = self.client.patch("/api/v1/me/", {"is_admin": True}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(elevate_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
        self.assertEqual(elevate_resp.data["error"]["code"], "field_not_allowed")

        # Verify in DB that is_admin is still False
        user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertFalse(user.is_admin)

    def test_verification_images_returns_metadata_only(self):
        user = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_user_images",
            display_name="Image Uploader",
        )
        token = issue_token_for_user(user)
        self.client.credentials(HTTP_AUTHORIZATION=f"Token {token.key}")

        # Upload image
        img_file = create_dummy_image_file()
        upload_resp = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/me/verification-images/",
            {"image_type": IMAGE_MOCK_SELFIE, "image": img_file},
            format="multipart",
        )
        self.assertEqual(upload_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_201_CREATED)

        # Assert metadata is present
        self.assertIn("id", upload_resp.data)
        self.assertIn("image_type", upload_resp.data)
        self.assertIn("file_sha256", upload_resp.data)
        self.assertIn("file_format", upload_resp.data)
        self.assertIn("status", upload_resp.data)

        # Assert RAW PATH IS NOT EXPOSED
        self.assertNotIn("image_path", upload_resp.data)
        self.assertNotIn("path", upload_resp.data)

        # GET images list also returns metadata only
        list_resp = self.client.get("/api/v1/me/verification-images/")
        self.assertEqual(list_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(len(list_resp.data), 1)
        self.assertNotIn("image_path", list_resp.data[0])

    def test_me_profile_unauthenticated_fails(self):
        resp = self.client.get("/api/v1/me/")
        self.assertIn(resp.status_code, [status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN])

    def test_me_charity_interests_get_and_put(self):
        f1 = CharityField.objects.create(code="elderly", label="Elderly Care", sort_order=1)
        user = User.objects.create(phone_hash="hash_user_interests", display_name="Interest User")
        token = issue_token_for_user(user)
        self.client.credentials(HTTP_AUTHORIZATION=f"Token {token.key}")

        put_resp = self.client.put("/api/v1/me/charity-interests/", {"codes": ["elderly"]})
        self.assertEqual(put_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(put_resp.data["interests"], ["elderly"])

        get_resp = self.client.get("/api/v1/me/charity-interests/")
        self.assertEqual(get_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(get_resp.data["interests"], ["elderly"])

    def test_me_coordinator_apply(self):
        user = User.objects.create(phone_hash="hash_coord_user", display_name="Coord Applicant")
        token = issue_token_for_user(user)
        self.client.credentials(HTTP_AUTHORIZATION=f"Token {token.key}")

        apply_resp = self.client.post("/api/v1/me/coordinator/", {"notes": "Experienced leader"})
        self.assertEqual(apply_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(apply_resp.data["status"], COORDINATOR_PENDING)
        self.assertEqual(apply_resp.data["notes"], "Experienced leader")

# apps/accounts/tests/test_auth.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.test import TestCase
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework.authtoken.models import Token

from apps.accounts.authentication import BlockedAwareTokenAuthentication
from apps.accounts.models import AuthOtp, User
from apps.accounts.services import request_otp, verify_otp
from apps.common.constants import ACCOUNT_ACTIVE, ACCOUNT_BLOCKED, ACCOUNT_CLOSED
from apps.common.exceptions import (
    AccountBlockedError,
    AccountClosedError,
    InvalidOtpError,
    TooManyAttemptsError,
)
from apps.common.security import compute_phone_hash, hash_otp


class AuthServicesTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.phone = "+959123456789"
        self.phone_hash = compute_phone_hash(self.phone)

    def test_otp_request_and_verify_creates_user(self):
        # Request OTP
        req_res = request_otp(self.phone, purpose="login")
        self.assertEqual(req_res.get("status"), "sent")
        code = req_res.get("dev_code")
        self.assertIsNotNone(code)

        # Verify OTP creates user and token
        user, token, created = verify_otp(self.phone, code, purpose="login")
        self.assertTrue(created)
        self.assertEqual(user.phone_hash, self.phone_hash)
        self.assertEqual(user.account_status, ACCOUNT_ACTIVE)
        self.assertIsNotNone(token)
        self.assertEqual(token.user, user)

        # Second verify for same user should not create a new user
        req_res2 = request_otp(self.phone, purpose="login")
        code2 = req_res2.get("dev_code")
        user2, token2, created2 = verify_otp(self.phone, code2, purpose="login")
        self.assertFalse(created2)
        self.assertEqual(user2.id, user.id)
        self.assertEqual(token2.key, token.key)

    def test_otp_reuse_fails(self):
        req_res = request_otp(self.phone, purpose="login")
        code = req_res.get("dev_code")

        # First verification succeeds
        verify_otp(self.phone, code, purpose="login")

        # Reusing the consumed OTP must raise InvalidOtpError
        with self.assertRaises(InvalidOtpError):
            verify_otp(self.phone, code, purpose="login")

    def test_otp_expired_fails(self):
        code = "123456"
        # Create an expired OTP directly
        AuthOtp.objects.create(
            phone_hash=self.phone_hash,
            code_hash=hash_otp(code),
            purpose="login",
            expires_at=timezone.now() - timedelta(seconds=10),
        )

        with self.assertRaises(InvalidOtpError):
            verify_otp(self.phone, code, purpose="login")

    def test_otp_max_attempts_locks(self):
        req_res = request_otp(self.phone, purpose="login")
        code = req_res.get("dev_code")
        wrong_code = "000000" if code != "000000" else "111111"

        # Fail 5 times with wrong code
        for _ in range(5):
            with self.assertRaises(InvalidOtpError):
                verify_otp(self.phone, wrong_code, purpose="login")

        # 6th attempt with correct code should fail with TooManyAttemptsError
        with self.assertRaises(TooManyAttemptsError):
            verify_otp(self.phone, code, purpose="login")

    def test_blocked_user_rejected_by_auth_class(self):
        # Create a blocked user and token
        user = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash=self.phone_hash,
            display_name="Blocked User",
            account_status=ACCOUNT_BLOCKED,
            blocked_until=timezone.now() + timedelta(days=1),
        )
        token = Token.objects.create(user=user)

        authenticator = BlockedAwareTokenAuthentication()

        with self.assertRaises(AccountBlockedError):
            authenticator.authenticate_credentials(token.key)

    def test_closed_account_cannot_request_otp(self):
        User.objects.create(
            phone_hash=self.phone_hash,
            display_name="Closed User",
            account_status=ACCOUNT_CLOSED,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(AccountClosedError):
            request_otp(self.phone, purpose="login")

# apps/accounts/tests/test_models.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db import IntegrityError
from django.db.models import Q
from django.test import SimpleTestCase
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import (
    AuthOtp,
    CoordinatorProfile,
    User,
    UserCharityInterest,
    UserVerificationImage,
)
from apps.common.constants import (
    ACCOUNT_ACTIVE,
    ACCOUNT_BLOCKED,
    COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
    COORDINATOR_PENDING,
    IMAGE_MOCK_SELFIE,
    VERIFICATION_PENDING,
    VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
)


class AccountsModelConstraintTests(SimpleTestCase):
    def test_blocked_until_constraint_violation(self):
        # 1. Active account with blocked_until MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_user = User(
            phone_hash="hash_blocked_until_violation",
            phone_enc="enc_phone",
            display_name="Invalid Blocked User",
            account_status=ACCOUNT_ACTIVE,
            blocked_until=timezone.now() + timedelta(days=1),
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_user.clean()

        # 2. Blocked account with blocked_until is valid
        valid_blocked_user = User(
            phone_hash="hash_valid_blocked",
            phone_enc="enc_phone",
            display_name="Valid Blocked User",
            account_status=ACCOUNT_BLOCKED,
            blocked_until=timezone.now() + timedelta(days=1),
        )
        # Should not raise validation error for blocked_until
        try:
            valid_blocked_user.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("blocked_until", e.message_dict)

        # 3. Active account with blocked_until=None is valid
        valid_active_user = User(
            phone_hash="hash_valid_active",
            phone_enc="enc_phone",
            display_name="Valid Active User",
            account_status=ACCOUNT_ACTIVE,
            blocked_until=None,
        )
        try:
            valid_active_user.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("blocked_until", e.message_dict)

        # Verify the CheckConstraint condition in Meta
        constraint = next(
            c for c in User._meta.constraints if c.name == "ck_users_blocked_until"
        )
        self.assertIsNotNone(constraint)
        self.assertEqual(
            constraint.condition,
            Q(blocked_until__isnull=True) | Q(account_status="blocked"),
        )

    def test_donation_consistency_constraint_violation(self):
        # 1. Willing to donate blood without is_willing_to_donate=True MUST fail
        invalid_blood_donor = User(
            phone_hash="hash_invalid_blood_donor",
            phone_enc="enc_phone",
            display_name="Invalid Blood Donor",
            is_willing_to_donate=False,
            is_willing_to_donate_blood=True,
            is_willing_to_donate_items=False,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_blood_donor.clean()

        # 2. Willing to donate items without is_willing_to_donate=True MUST fail
        invalid_items_donor = User(
            phone_hash="hash_invalid_items_donor",
            phone_enc="enc_phone",
            display_name="Invalid Items Donor",
            is_willing_to_donate=False,
            is_willing_to_donate_blood=False,
            is_willing_to_donate_items=True,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_items_donor.clean()

        # 3. Willing to donate=True with blood/items is valid
        valid_donor = User(
            phone_hash="hash_valid_donor",
            phone_enc="enc_phone",
            display_name="Valid Donor",
            is_willing_to_donate=True,
            is_willing_to_donate_blood=True,
            is_willing_to_donate_items=True,
        )
        try:
            valid_donor.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("is_willing_to_donate", e.message_dict)

        # 4. Willing to donate=False with blood=False, items=False is valid
        valid_non_donor = User(
            phone_hash="hash_valid_non_donor",
            phone_enc="enc_phone",
            display_name="Valid Non Donor",
            is_willing_to_donate=False,
            is_willing_to_donate_blood=False,
            is_willing_to_donate_items=False,
        )
        try:
            valid_non_donor.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("is_willing_to_donate", e.message_dict)

        # Verify the CheckConstraint condition in Meta
        constraint = next(
            c for c in User._meta.constraints if c.name == "ck_users_donation_consistency"
        )
        self.assertIsNotNone(constraint)
        self.assertEqual(
            constraint.condition,
            Q(is_willing_to_donate=True)
            | (Q(is_willing_to_donate_blood=False) & Q(is_willing_to_donate_items=False)),
        )

    def test_single_current_verification_image_constraint(self):
        # Verify UniqueConstraint specification on UserVerificationImage
        constraint = next(
            c for c in UserVerificationImage._meta.constraints
            if c.name == "uq_current_verification_image"
        )
        self.assertIsNotNone(constraint)
        self.assertEqual(list(constraint.fields), ["user", "image_type"])
        self.assertEqual(constraint.condition, Q(is_current=True))

        # Also verify review constraint on UserVerificationImage
        review_constraint = next(
            c for c in UserVerificationImage._meta.constraints
            if c.name == "ck_verification_review"
        )
        self.assertIsNotNone(review_constraint)
        self.assertEqual(
            review_constraint.condition,
            Q(status="pending") | (Q(reviewed_by__isnull=False) & Q(reviewed_at__isnull=False)),
        )

        # A verified image without reviewed_by/reviewed_at MUST fail validation
        dummy_user = User(id=1, phone_hash="dummy_u1", display_name="Dummy User")
        invalid_verified_image = UserVerificationImage(
            user=dummy_user,
            image_type=IMAGE_MOCK_SELFIE,
            status=VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
            reviewed_by=None,
            reviewed_at=None,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_verified_image.clean()

    def test_coordinator_approval_timestamp_constraint(self):
        dummy_user = User(id=2, phone_hash="dummy_u2", display_name="Coordinator User")

        # 1. Approved coordinator without approved_at MUST fail validation
        invalid_approved_profile = CoordinatorProfile(
            user=dummy_user,
            status=COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
            approved_at=None,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_approved_profile.clean()

        # 2. Pending coordinator without approved_at is valid
        valid_pending_profile = CoordinatorProfile(
            user=dummy_user,
            status=COORDINATOR_PENDING,
            approved_at=None,
        )
        try:
            valid_pending_profile.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("approved_at", e.message_dict)

        # 3. Approved coordinator with approved_at is valid
        valid_approved_profile = CoordinatorProfile(
            user=dummy_user,
            status=COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
            approved_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        try:
            valid_approved_profile.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("approved_at", e.message_dict)

        # Verify the CheckConstraint condition in Meta
        constraint = next(
            c for c in CoordinatorProfile._meta.constraints
            if c.name == "ck_coordinator_approval"
        )
        self.assertIsNotNone(constraint)
        self.assertEqual(
            constraint.condition,
            Q(status="pending") | Q(approved_at__isnull=False),
        )

# apps/accounts/tests/test_profile_services.py
from io import BytesIO
from PIL import Image
from django.core.files.uploadedfile import SimpleUploadedFile
from django.test import TestCase

from apps.accounts.models import CoordinatorProfile, User, UserVerificationImage
from apps.accounts.services import (
    add_verification_image,
    apply_as_coordinator,
    approve_coordinator,
    recompute_verification_status,
    review_verification_image,
    revoke_coordinator,
    set_charity_interests,
    suspend_coordinator,
    update_profile,
)
from apps.common.constants import (
    COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
    COORDINATOR_PENDING,
    COORDINATOR_REVOKED,
    COORDINATOR_SUSPENDED,
    IMAGE_DRIVER_LICENSE,
    IMAGE_ID_LICENSE,
    IMAGE_MOCK_SELFIE,
    VERIFICATION_NONE,
    VERIFICATION_PENDING,
    VERIFICATION_REJECTED,
    VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
)
from apps.common.exceptions import (
    DomainError,
    PermissionDeniedError,
    VerificationRequiredError,
)
from apps.events.models import CharityField


def create_dummy_uploaded_image(filename="sample.png", format="PNG", color="red"):
    buffer = BytesIO()
    image = Image.new("RGBA", (100, 100), color=color)
    image.save(buffer, format=format)
    buffer.seek(0)
    return SimpleUploadedFile(filename, buffer.getvalue(), content_type=f"image/{format.lower()}")


class ProfileServicesTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.user = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_user_1",
            display_name="Volunteer One",
        )
        self.admin = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_admin",
            display_name="Admin One",
            is_admin=True,
        )

    def test_verification_image_transcoded_to_jpeg_and_old_deactivated(self):
        # 1. Upload first selfie (PNG format)
        file1 = create_dummy_uploaded_image("selfie1.png", format="PNG", color="blue")
        img1 = add_verification_image(self.user, IMAGE_MOCK_SELFIE, file1)

        self.assertTrue(img1.is_current)
        self.assertEqual(img1.file_format, "jpeg")
        self.assertTrue(img1.image_path.name.endswith(".jpg"))
        self.assertIsNotNone(img1.file_sha256)

        # User status should now be pending
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.verification_status, VERIFICATION_PENDING)

        # 2. Upload second selfie
        file2 = create_dummy_uploaded_image("selfie2.png", format="PNG", color="green")
        img2 = add_verification_image(self.user, IMAGE_MOCK_SELFIE, file2)

        # img1 must now be deactivated (is_current=False), img2 is current
        img1.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertFalse(img1.is_current)
        self.assertTrue(img2.is_current)
        self.assertNotEqual(img1.id, img2.id)

    def test_recompute_verification_status_logic(self):
        # Initially none
        self.assertEqual(self.user.verification_status, VERIFICATION_NONE)

        # Upload selfie -> pending
        file_selfie = create_dummy_uploaded_image("selfie.png")
        img_selfie = add_verification_image(self.user, IMAGE_MOCK_SELFIE, file_selfie)
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.verification_status, VERIFICATION_PENDING)

        # Upload ID -> pending
        file_id = create_dummy_uploaded_image("id.png")
        img_id = add_verification_image(self.user, IMAGE_ID_LICENSE, file_id)
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.verification_status, VERIFICATION_PENDING)

        # Approve selfie only -> still pending because ID is pending
        review_verification_image(self.admin, img_selfie, status="approved")
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.verification_status, VERIFICATION_PENDING)

        # Approve ID -> both approved -> verified!
        review_verification_image(self.admin, img_id, status="approved")
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.verification_status, VERIFICATION_VERIFIED)

        # If a new ID is uploaded and rejected -> status becomes rejected
        file_id2 = create_dummy_uploaded_image("id2.png")
        img_id2 = add_verification_image(self.user, IMAGE_ID_LICENSE, file_id2)
        review_verification_image(self.admin, img_id2, status="rejected", note="Blurry image")
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.verification_status, VERIFICATION_REJECTED)

    def test_coordinator_approval_requires_verified_user(self):
        # Apply as coordinator
        profile = apply_as_coordinator(self.user, notes="Ready to lead events")
        self.assertEqual(profile.status, COORDINATOR_PENDING)

        # Non-admin cannot approve
        non_admin = User.objects.create(phone_hash="hash_regular", display_name="Regular")
        with self.assertRaises(PermissionDeniedError):
            approve_coordinator(non_admin, profile)

        # Admin cannot approve if user is unverified
        self.assertEqual(self.user.verification_status, VERIFICATION_NONE)
        with self.assertRaises(VerificationRequiredError):
            approve_coordinator(self.admin, profile)

        # Now verify user
        self.user.verification_status = VERIFICATION_VERIFIED
        self.user.save()

        # Approval succeeds
        approved_profile = approve_coordinator(self.admin, profile)
        self.assertEqual(approved_profile.status, COORDINATOR_APPROVED)
        self.assertEqual(approved_profile.approved_by, self.admin)
        self.assertIsNotNone(approved_profile.approved_at)

        # Suspend
        suspended = suspend_coordinator(self.admin, approved_profile)
        self.assertEqual(suspended.status, COORDINATOR_SUSPENDED)

        # Revoke
        revoked = revoke_coordinator(self.admin, suspended)
        self.assertEqual(revoked.status, COORDINATOR_REVOKED)

    def test_update_profile_disallows_restricted_fields(self):
        # Updating regular fields works
        update_profile(self.user, {"display_name": "New Name", "home_area": "Downtown"})
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.display_name, "New Name")
        self.assertEqual(self.user.home_area, "Downtown")

        # Updating restricted fields raises DomainError
        with self.assertRaises(DomainError):
            update_profile(self.user, {"is_admin": True})

        with self.assertRaises(DomainError):
            update_profile(self.user, {"verification_status": VERIFICATION_VERIFIED})

    def test_set_charity_interests_atomic_replace(self):
        f1 = CharityField.objects.create(code="education", label="Education", sort_order=1)
        f2 = CharityField.objects.create(code="healthcare", label="Healthcare", sort_order=2)
        f3 = CharityField.objects.create(code="animals", label="Animal Welfare", sort_order=3)

        set_charity_interests(self.user, ["education", "healthcare"])
        interests = list(self.user.charity_interests.values_list("field_code", flat=True))
        self.assertCountEqual(interests, ["education", "healthcare"])

        # Replaces cleanly
        set_charity_interests(self.user, ["animals"])
        interests2 = list(self.user.charity_interests.values_list("field_code", flat=True))
        self.assertCountEqual(interests2, ["animals"])

# apps/audit/tests/test_api.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.test import APITestCase

from apps.accounts.models import CoordinatorProfile, User
from apps.audit.models import ModerationAction
from apps.common.constants import COORDINATOR_APPROVED, VERIFICATION_VERIFIED
from apps.events.services import add_post, create_event, publish_event


class AuditApiTests(APITestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        # 1. Admin
        self.admin = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_admin_api",
            display_name="API Admin",
            is_admin=True,
        )

        # 2. Regular user
        self.user = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_user_api",
            display_name="API Volunteer",
            account_status="active",
        )

        # 3. Coordinator & Event
        self.coordinator = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_coord_api_mod",
            display_name="API Coordinator",
            verification_status=VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
        )
        CoordinatorProfile.objects.create(
            user=self.coordinator,
            status=COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
            approved_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        now = timezone.now()
        self.event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Riverside Tree Planting",
                "start_at": now + timedelta(days=2),
                "end_at": now + timedelta(days=2, hours=4),
            },
        )
        self.post = add_post(
            self.coordinator,
            self.event,
            {"title": "Planter", "required_headcount": 5},
        )
        publish_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

    def test_dashboard_stats_endpoint_admin_only(self):
        # 1. Anonymous forbidden / unauthorized
        resp_anon = self.client.get("/api/v1/admin/dashboard/")
        self.assertIn(resp_anon.status_code, [status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN])

        # 2. Regular user forbidden
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.user)
        resp_user = self.client.get("/api/v1/admin/dashboard/")
        self.assertEqual(resp_user.status_code, status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        # 3. Admin allowed
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin)
        resp_admin = self.client.get("/api/v1/admin/dashboard/")
        self.assertEqual(resp_admin.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertIn("active_users", resp_admin.data)
        self.assertIn("events_published", resp_admin.data)

    def test_block_user_endpoint_executes_service(self):
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin)

        resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/admin/users/{self.user.id}/block/",
            {"reason": "Terms violation."},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(resp.data["action_type"], "user_block")

        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.account_status, "blocked")

        # Unblock user
        resp_unblock = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/admin/users/{self.user.id}/unblock/",
            {"reason": "Appeal accepted."},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(resp_unblock.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.account_status, "active")

    def test_event_delete_and_restore_api(self):
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin)

        # Delete event
        del_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/admin/events/{self.event.id}/delete/",
            {"reason": "Content violation."},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(del_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.event.deleted_at)

        # Restore event
        res_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/admin/events/{self.event.id}/restore/",
            {"reason": "Resolved."},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(res_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNone(self.event.deleted_at)

    def test_user_warn_close_reinstate_api(self):
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin)

        # 1. Warn user
        warn_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/admin/users/{self.user.id}/warn/",
            {"reason": "Inappropriate comment warning."},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(warn_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(warn_resp.data["action_type"], "user_warning")

        # 2. Close account
        close_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/admin/users/{self.user.id}/close/",
            {"reason": "Severe policy violation."},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(close_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.account_status, "closed")

        # 3. Reinstate account
        rein_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/admin/users/{self.user.id}/reinstate/",
            {"reason": "Account reinstated after review."},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(rein_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.account_status, "active")

    def test_event_cancel_and_feedback_hide_api(self):
        from apps.events.services import activate_event, complete_event
        from apps.ops.models import Feedback
        from apps.ops.services import create_assignment_from_application, submit_feedback
        from apps.events.models import EventApplication

        # Create participating assignment and completed event for feedback
        app = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            post=self.post,
            user=self.user,
            status="accepted",
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        create_assignment_from_application(app)
        activate_event(self.coordinator, self.event)
        complete_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

        fb = submit_feedback(
            user=self.user,
            event=self.event,
            direction="volunteer_to_event",
            score=1,
            comment="Bad language content.",
        )

        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.admin)

        # Hide feedback
        hide_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/admin/feedback/{fb.id}/hide/",
            {"reason": "Profanity detected."},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(hide_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        fb.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(fb.status, "hidden")

        # Create another event to test cancel
        now = timezone.now()
        event2 = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {"title": "Flood Event", "start_at": now + timedelta(days=2), "end_at": now + timedelta(days=2, hours=3)},
        )
        add_post(self.coordinator, event2, {"title": "Relief Post", "required_headcount": 5})
        publish_event(self.coordinator, event2)

        cancel_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/admin/events/{event2.id}/cancel/",
            {"reason": "Cancelled due to river overflow risk."},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(cancel_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        event2.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(event2.status, "cancelled")

# apps/audit/tests/test_models.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db.models import Q
from django.test import SimpleTestCase
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import User
from apps.audit.models import AuditLog, ModerationAction
from apps.events.models import Event
from apps.ops.models import Feedback


class AuditModelConstraintTests(SimpleTestCase):
    def _create_dummy_user(self, user_id=1):
        return User(id=user_id, phone_hash=f"hash_{user_id}", display_name=f"User {user_id}")

    def test_audit_log_append_only(self):
        actor = self._create_dummy_user(1)
        log = AuditLog(
            id=10,
            actor_user=actor,
            action="user_created",
            entity_type="user",
            entity_id=1,
        )
        # Mark as already persisted
        log._state.adding = False

        # 1. Updating existing AuditLog MUST raise ValidationError
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError) as ctx:
            log.save()
        self.assertIn("append-only", str(ctx.exception))

        # 2. Deleting AuditLog MUST raise ValidationError
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError) as ctx:
            log.delete()
        self.assertIn("append-only", str(ctx.exception))

    def test_moderation_action_subject_validation(self):
        admin_user = self._create_dummy_user(99)
        target_user = self._create_dummy_user(10)
        event = Event(id=1, title="Test Event")
        feedback = Feedback(id=1, event=event, rater_user=target_user)

        # 1. User action without target_user MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_user_mod = ModerationAction(
            admin_user=admin_user,
            action_type="user_block",
            target_user=None,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_user_mod.clean()

        # User action with target_user is valid
        valid_user_mod = ModerationAction(
            admin_user=admin_user,
            action_type="user_warning",
            target_user=target_user,
        )
        try:
            valid_user_mod.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("target_user", str(e))

        # 2. Event action without event MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_event_mod = ModerationAction(
            admin_user=admin_user,
            action_type="event_cancel",
            event=None,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_event_mod.clean()

        # Event action with event is valid
        valid_event_mod = ModerationAction(
            admin_user=admin_user,
            action_type="event_delete",
            event=event,
        )
        try:
            valid_event_mod.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("event", str(e))

        # 3. Feedback action without feedback MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_feedback_mod = ModerationAction(
            admin_user=admin_user,
            action_type="feedback_hide",
            feedback=None,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_feedback_mod.clean()

        # Feedback action with feedback is valid
        valid_feedback_mod = ModerationAction(
            admin_user=admin_user,
            action_type="feedback_hide",
            feedback=feedback,
        )
        try:
            valid_feedback_mod.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("feedback", str(e))

        # 4. Verify CheckConstraint in Meta
        subject_constraint = next(
            c for c in ModerationAction._meta.constraints
            if c.name == "ck_moderation_subject"
        )
        self.assertIsNotNone(subject_constraint)

    def test_moderation_action_expiry_only_on_user_block(self):
        admin_user = self._create_dummy_user(99)
        target_user = self._create_dummy_user(10)
        event = Event(id=1, title="Test Event")
        now = timezone.now()

        # 1. user_block with expires_at is valid
        valid_expiring_block = ModerationAction(
            admin_user=admin_user,
            action_type="user_block",
            target_user=target_user,
            expires_at=now + timedelta(days=7),
        )
        try:
            valid_expiring_block.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("expires_at", str(e))

        # 2. user_warning with expires_at MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_warning = ModerationAction(
            admin_user=admin_user,
            action_type="user_warning",
            target_user=target_user,
            expires_at=now + timedelta(days=7),
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_warning.clean()

        # 3. event_cancel with expires_at MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_event_cancel = ModerationAction(
            admin_user=admin_user,
            action_type="event_cancel",
            event=event,
            expires_at=now + timedelta(days=7),
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_event_cancel.clean()

        # 4. Verify CheckConstraint in Meta
        expiry_constraint = next(
            c for c in ModerationAction._meta.constraints
            if c.name == "ck_moderation_expiry"
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            expiry_constraint.condition,
            Q(expires_at__isnull=True) | Q(action_type="user_block"),
        )

# apps/audit/tests/test_moderation.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.test import TestCase
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import CoordinatorProfile, User
from apps.audit.models import AuditLog, ModerationAction
from apps.audit.services import (
    block_user,
    close_account,
    dashboard_stats,
    delete_event,
    hide_feedback,
    reinstate_account,
    restore_event,
    unblock_user,
    warn_user,
)
from apps.common.constants import (
    COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
    FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT,
    VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
)
from apps.common.exceptions import PermissionDeniedError
from apps.events.models import Event
from apps.events.services import (
    activate_event,
    add_post,
    complete_event,
    create_event,
    publish_event,
)
from apps.ops.models import Feedback
from apps.ops.services import (
    create_assignment_from_application,
    submit_feedback,
)
from apps.events.models import EventApplication


class ModerationServicesTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        # 1. Setup Admin
        self.admin = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_admin_mod",
            display_name="Super Admin",
            is_admin=True,
        )

        # 2. Setup regular user
        self.user = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_regular_user",
            display_name="Regular Volunteer",
            account_status="active",
        )

        # 3. Setup coordinator & event
        self.coordinator = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_coord_mod",
            display_name="Coordinator Mod",
            verification_status=VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
        )
        CoordinatorProfile.objects.create(
            user=self.coordinator,
            status=COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
            approved_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        now = timezone.now()
        self.event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Clean River Event",
                "start_at": now + timedelta(days=3),
                "end_at": now + timedelta(days=3, hours=5),
            },
        )
        self.post = add_post(
            self.coordinator,
            self.event,
            {"title": "Water Collector", "required_headcount": 5},
        )
        publish_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

    def test_block_user_updates_state_and_writes_moderation_and_audit(self):
        expires_at = timezone.now() + timedelta(days=7)
        mod_action = block_user(
            admin=self.admin,
            target_user=self.user,
            reason="Violation of safety policies.",
            expires_at=expires_at,
        )

        # Check user state
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.account_status, "blocked")
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.user.blocked_until)

        # Check moderation action
        self.assertEqual(mod_action.action_type, "user_block")
        self.assertEqual(mod_action.admin_user, self.admin)
        self.assertEqual(mod_action.target_user, self.user)
        self.assertEqual(mod_action.reason, "Violation of safety policies.")

        # Check audit log written
        audit = AuditLog.objects.filter(entity_type="user", entity_id=self.user.id, action="block_user").first()
        self.assertIsNotNone(audit)
        self.assertEqual(audit.actor_user, self.admin)
        self.assertEqual(audit.after_data["account_status"], "blocked")

    def test_delete_event_sets_soft_delete_and_hides_from_queries(self):
        mod_action = delete_event(
            admin=self.admin,
            event=self.event,
            reason="Event violates terms of service.",
        )

        self.event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNotNone(self.event.deleted_at)
        self.assertEqual(self.event.deleted_by, self.admin)

        # Event visible() queryset hides soft-deleted events
        visible_events = Event.objects.visible()
        self.assertNotIn(self.event, visible_events)

        # Restore event
        restore_event(admin=self.admin, event=self.event, reason="Resolved appeal.")
        self.event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertIsNone(self.event.deleted_at)
        self.assertIsNone(self.event.deleted_by)
        self.assertIn(self.event, Event.objects.visible())

    def test_non_admin_cannot_execute_moderation(self):
        with self.assertRaises(PermissionDeniedError):
            block_user(admin=self.user, target_user=self.coordinator, reason="Unauthorized block")

        with self.assertRaises(PermissionDeniedError):
            delete_event(admin=self.user, event=self.event, reason="Unauthorized delete")

        with self.assertRaises(PermissionDeniedError):
            warn_user(admin=self.user, target_user=self.coordinator, reason="Unauthorized warn")

    def test_user_lifecycle_moderations(self):
        # 1. Warn
        warn = warn_user(self.admin, self.user, reason="Minor warning.")
        self.assertEqual(warn.action_type, "user_warning")

        # 2. Block and Unblock
        block_user(self.admin, self.user, reason="Temp block.")
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.account_status, "blocked")

        unblock_user(self.admin, self.user, reason="Appeal granted.")
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.account_status, "active")

        # 3. Close and Reinstate
        close_account(self.admin, self.user, reason="Permanent violation.")
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.account_status, "closed")

        reinstate_account(self.admin, self.user, reason="Reinstated after review.")
        self.user.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.user.account_status, "active")

    def test_hide_feedback_and_dashboard_stats(self):
        app = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            post=self.post,
            user=self.user,
            status="accepted",
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        create_assignment_from_application(app)
        activate_event(self.coordinator, self.event)
        complete_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

        fb = submit_feedback(
            user=self.user,
            event=self.event,
            direction=FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT,
            score=1,
            comment="Inappropriate comment content.",
        )

        mod = hide_feedback(self.admin, fb, reason="Contains abusive language.")
        self.assertEqual(mod.action_type, "feedback_hide")
        fb.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(fb.status, "hidden")

        stats = dashboard_stats()
        self.assertIn("active_users", stats)
        self.assertIn("blocked_users", stats)
        self.assertIn("events_completed", stats)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(stats["events_completed"], 1)

    def test_cancel_event_by_admin(self):
        from apps.audit.services import cancel_event_by_admin
        mod = cancel_event_by_admin(self.admin, self.event, reason="Severe flood warning.")
        self.assertEqual(mod.action_type, "event_cancel")
        self.assertEqual(mod.admin_user, self.admin)
        self.event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(self.event.status, "cancelled")
