# apps/common/tests/test_security.py
from django.test import SimpleTestCase, override_settings

from apps.common.security import (
    compute_phone_hash,
    decrypt_phone,
    encrypt_phone,
    generate_otp_code,
    generate_raw_token,
    hash_otp,
    hash_token,
    normalize_phone,
)


@override_settings(PHONE_HASH_HMAC_SECRET="test-hmac-secret-volunteer-system-2026")
class SecurityTests(SimpleTestCase):
    def test_phone_normalization_formats(self):
        # International with plus and spaces/dashes
        self.assertEqual(normalize_phone("+1 (555) 123-4567"), "+15551234567")
        self.assertEqual(normalize_phone("  +44 20 7946 0958  "), "+442079460958")
        self.assertEqual(normalize_phone("+95-9-123-456-789"), "+959123456789")

        # Local without plus
        self.assertEqual(normalize_phone("09-123-456-789"), "09123456789")
        self.assertEqual(normalize_phone(" (09) 1234 5678 "), "0912345678")

        # Empty / None handling
        self.assertEqual(normalize_phone(""), "")
        self.assertEqual(normalize_phone(None), "")

    def test_phone_hash_is_deterministic(self):
        # Same phone yields same hash
        h1 = compute_phone_hash("+959123456789")
        h2 = compute_phone_hash("+959123456789")
        self.assertEqual(h1, h2)
        self.assertEqual(len(h1), 64)  # HMAC-SHA256 hex digest length

        # Formatted phone yields identical hash due to normalization
        h3 = compute_phone_hash("+95-9-123-456-789")
        self.assertEqual(h1, h3)

        # Different phones yield different hashes
        h4 = compute_phone_hash("+959987654321")
        self.assertNotEqual(h1, h4)

    def test_encrypt_decrypt_phone_roundtrip(self):
        raw_phone = "+959123456789"
        encrypted = encrypt_phone(raw_phone)

        # Encrypted string must not be plain text
        self.assertNotEqual(encrypted, raw_phone)
        self.assertNotIn("959123456789", encrypted)

        # Decryption restores the normalized phone
        decrypted = decrypt_phone(encrypted)
        self.assertEqual(decrypted, raw_phone)

        # Test phone with spaces/dashes restores normalized form
        formatted_phone = "+1 (555) 234-5678"
        enc_formatted = encrypt_phone(formatted_phone)
        dec_formatted = decrypt_phone(enc_formatted)
        self.assertEqual(dec_formatted, "+15552345678")

    def test_raw_token_does_not_equal_hash(self):
        raw_token = generate_raw_token()
        self.assertTrue(len(raw_token) >= 32)

        hashed = hash_token(raw_token)
        self.assertEqual(len(hashed), 64)  # SHA-256 hex digest
        self.assertNotEqual(raw_token, hashed)

        # Hash is deterministic
        self.assertEqual(hash_token(raw_token), hashed)

    def test_hash_otp_deterministic(self):
        otp = generate_otp_code()
        self.assertEqual(len(otp), 6)
        self.assertTrue(otp.isdigit())

        h1 = hash_otp(otp)
        h2 = hash_otp(otp)
        self.assertEqual(h1, h2)
        self.assertEqual(len(h1), 64)
        self.assertNotEqual(otp, h1)

        # Different OTP codes yield different hashes
        diff_otp = "000000" if otp != "000000" else "111111"
        self.assertNotEqual(hash_otp(otp), hash_otp(diff_otp))

# apps/events/tests/test_api.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.test import APITestCase

from apps.accounts.models import CoordinatorProfile, User
from apps.accounts.services import issue_token_for_user
from apps.common.constants import (
    APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
    APPLICATION_PENDING,
    COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
    EVENT_ACTIVE,
    EVENT_DRAFT,
    EVENT_PUBLISHED,
    VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
)
from apps.events.models import Event, EventApplication, EventPost
from apps.events.services import add_post, create_event, publish_event


class EventsApiTests(APITestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        # 1. Setup approved coordinator
        self.coordinator = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_coord_api",
            display_name="Coord Api",
            verification_status=VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
        )
        CoordinatorProfile.objects.create(
            user=self.coordinator,
            status=COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
            approved_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        self.coord_token = issue_token_for_user(self.coordinator)

        # 2. Setup volunteer
        self.volunteer = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_vol_api",
            display_name="Vol Api",
            physical_ok=True,
            skills=["first_aid"],
        )
        self.vol_token = issue_token_for_user(self.volunteer)

        now = timezone.now()
        self.start_at = now + timedelta(days=5)
        self.end_at = self.start_at + timedelta(hours=4)

    def test_public_events_list_hides_draft_and_deleted(self):
        # Create a published event
        pub_event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Visible Published Event",
                "start_at": self.start_at,
                "end_at": self.end_at,
            },
        )
        add_post(self.coordinator, pub_event, {"title": "Post 1", "required_headcount": 5})
        publish_event(self.coordinator, pub_event)

        # Create a draft event
        draft_event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Hidden Draft Event",
                "start_at": self.start_at,
                "end_at": self.end_at,
            },
        )

        # Create a deleted published event
        del_event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Hidden Deleted Event",
                "start_at": self.start_at,
                "end_at": self.end_at,
                "deleted_at": timezone.now(),
                "deleted_by": self.coordinator,
            },
        )

        # Fetch public list (unauthenticated)
        resp = self.client.get("/api/v1/events/")
        self.assertEqual(resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)

        titles = [e["title"] for e in resp.data]
        self.assertIn("Visible Published Event", titles)
        self.assertNotIn("Hidden Draft Event", titles)
        self.assertNotIn("Hidden Deleted Event", titles)

    def test_coordinator_publish_action_endpoint(self):
        # 1. Coordinator creates draft event
        self.client.credentials(HTTP_AUTHORIZATION=f"Token {self.coord_token.key}")
        create_resp = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/coordinator/events/",
            {
                "title": "New Coordinator Event",
                "start_at": self.start_at.isoformat(),
                "end_at": self.end_at.isoformat(),
            },
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(create_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
        event_id = create_resp.data["id"]
        self.assertEqual(create_resp.data["status"], EVENT_DRAFT)

        # 2. Attempt publish without post -> 400 DomainError (post_required)
        pub_fail = self.client.post(f"/api/v1/events/{event_id}/publish/")
        self.assertEqual(pub_fail.status_code, status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
        self.assertEqual(pub_fail.data["error"]["code"], "post_required")

        # 3. Add post
        post_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/events/{event_id}/posts/",
            {"title": "Helper", "required_headcount": 2},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(post_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_201_CREATED)

        # 4. Publish event succeeds
        pub_ok = self.client.post(f"/api/v1/events/{event_id}/publish/")
        self.assertEqual(pub_ok.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(pub_ok.data["status"], EVENT_PUBLISHED)

    def test_apply_endpoint_returns_application_status(self):
        # Setup published event
        event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Health Checkup",
                "start_at": self.start_at,
                "end_at": self.end_at,
                "acceptance_mode": "auto",
                "max_participants": 5,
            },
        )
        post = add_post(self.coordinator, event, {"title": "Registrar", "required_headcount": 2})
        publish_event(self.coordinator, event)

        # Volunteer applies via API
        self.client.credentials(HTTP_AUTHORIZATION=f"Token {self.vol_token.key}")
        apply_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/events/{event.id}/apply/",
            {"post_id": post.id, "source": "browse"},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(apply_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
        self.assertEqual(apply_resp.data["status"], APPLICATION_ACCEPTED)
        self.assertEqual(apply_resp.data["post"], post.id)
        self.assertEqual(apply_resp.data["event"], event.id)

    def test_application_accept_and_roster_endpoints(self):
        event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Manual Event",
                "start_at": self.start_at,
                "end_at": self.end_at,
                "acceptance_mode": "manual",
            },
        )
        post = add_post(self.coordinator, event, {"title": "Guide", "required_headcount": 2})
        publish_event(self.coordinator, event)

        # Volunteer applies -> status pending
        self.client.credentials(HTTP_AUTHORIZATION=f"Token {self.vol_token.key}")
        app_resp = self.client.post(f"/api/v1/events/{event.id}/apply/", {"post_id": post.id}, format="json")
        self.assertEqual(app_resp.data["status"], APPLICATION_PENDING)
        app_id = app_resp.data["id"]

        # Coordinator accepts application
        self.client.credentials(HTTP_AUTHORIZATION=f"Token {self.coord_token.key}")
        accept_resp = self.client.post(f"/api/v1/applications/{app_id}/accept/", format="json")
        self.assertEqual(accept_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(accept_resp.data["application"]["status"], APPLICATION_ACCEPTED)

        # Coordinator checks roster
        roster_resp = self.client.get(f"/api/v1/events/{event.id}/roster/")
        self.assertEqual(roster_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(len(roster_resp.data["roster"]), 1)
        self.assertEqual(roster_resp.data["roster"][0]["display_name"], self.volunteer.display_name)

# apps/events/tests/test_applications.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.test import TestCase
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import CoordinatorProfile, User
from apps.common.constants import (
    APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
    APPLICATION_PENDING,
    APPLICATION_WAITLISTED,
    APPLICATION_WITHDRAWN,
    ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,
    ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED,
    COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
    EVENT_ACTIVE,
    EVENT_PUBLISHED,
    VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
)
from apps.common.exceptions import CapacityFullError, DomainError
from apps.events.models import Event, EventApplication, EventPost
from apps.events.services import (
    accept_application,
    add_post,
    apply_to_event,
    create_event,
    publish_event,
    reject_application,
    withdraw_application,
)
from apps.ops.models import Assignment


class EventApplicationsTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.coordinator = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="coord_hash_apps",
            display_name="Coordinator Apps",
            verification_status=VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
        )
        CoordinatorProfile.objects.create(
            user=self.coordinator,
            status=COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
            approved_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        self.volunteer1 = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="vol_hash_1",
            display_name="Volunteer 1",
            physical_ok=True,
            can_lift_heavy=True,
            skills=["first_aid", "driving"],
        )
        self.volunteer2 = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="vol_hash_2",
            display_name="Volunteer 2",
            physical_ok=True,
            skills=["first_aid"],
        )

        now = timezone.now()
        self.event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Medical Camp",
                "start_at": now + timedelta(days=7),
                "end_at": now + timedelta(days=7, hours=5),
                "application_deadline": now + timedelta(days=3),
                "acceptance_mode": "auto",
                "max_participants": 2,
            },
        )
        self.post = add_post(
            self.coordinator,
            self.event,
            {
                "title": "First Aid Responder",
                "required_headcount": 1,
                "required_skills": ["first_aid"],
            },
        )
        publish_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

    def test_duplicate_active_application_blocked(self):
        # 1. Apply to event
        app1 = apply_to_event(self.volunteer1, self.event, post=self.post)
        self.assertEqual(app1.status, APPLICATION_ACCEPTED)

        # 2. Applying again must raise duplicate application error
        with self.assertRaises(DomainError) as ctx:
            apply_to_event(self.volunteer1, self.event, post=self.post)
        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.code, "duplicate_application")

    def test_auto_acceptance_with_capacity_creates_accepted(self):
        # Capacity is 1 on post, volunteer1 applies
        app = apply_to_event(self.volunteer1, self.event, post=self.post)
        self.assertEqual(app.status, APPLICATION_ACCEPTED)
        self.assertIsNotNone(app.decided_at)

        # Because auto_assign_enabled is True, assignment should be created automatically
        assignment = Assignment.objects.filter(application=app, user=self.volunteer1).first()
        self.assertIsNotNone(assignment)
        self.assertEqual(assignment.status, ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED)
        self.assertEqual(assignment.assigned_by, "auto")

    def test_auto_acceptance_full_capacity_waitlists(self):
        # Fill capacity
        app1 = apply_to_event(self.volunteer1, self.event, post=self.post)
        self.assertEqual(app1.status, APPLICATION_ACCEPTED)

        # Second volunteer applies for the same post whose capacity is 1
        app2 = apply_to_event(self.volunteer2, self.event, post=self.post)
        self.assertEqual(app2.status, APPLICATION_WAITLISTED)
        self.assertIsNotNone(app2.decided_at)

    def test_past_deadline_application_fails(self):
        # Set deadline to 1 hour in the past
        self.event.application_deadline = timezone.now() - timedelta(hours=1)
        self.event.save()

        with self.assertRaises(DomainError) as ctx:
            apply_to_event(self.volunteer1, self.event, post=self.post)
        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.code, "deadline_passed")

    def test_manual_application_and_accept_creates_assignment(self):
        # Set event to manual acceptance mode
        self.event.acceptance_mode = "manual"
        self.event.save()

        # Apply -> status should be pending
        app = apply_to_event(self.volunteer1, self.event, post=self.post)
        self.assertEqual(app.status, APPLICATION_PENDING)
        self.assertIsNone(app.decided_at)

        # Coordinator accepts application
        accepted_app, assignment = accept_application(self.coordinator, app)
        self.assertEqual(accepted_app.status, APPLICATION_ACCEPTED)
        self.assertEqual(accepted_app.decided_by, self.coordinator)
        self.assertIsNotNone(accepted_app.decided_at)
        self.assertIsNotNone(assignment)
        self.assertEqual(assignment.assigned_by, "coordinator")
        self.assertEqual(assignment.assigned_by_user, self.coordinator)

    def test_withdraw_application_cancels_assignment(self):
        app = apply_to_event(self.volunteer1, self.event, post=self.post)
        self.assertEqual(app.status, APPLICATION_ACCEPTED)
        assignment = Assignment.objects.get(application=app)

        # Volunteer withdraws
        withdrawn_app = withdraw_application(self.volunteer1, app)
        self.assertEqual(withdrawn_app.status, APPLICATION_WITHDRAWN)

        assignment.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(assignment.status, ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED)

# apps/events/tests/test_lifecycle.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.test import TestCase
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import CoordinatorProfile, User
from apps.common.constants import (
    ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,
    ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED,
    ASSIGNMENT_COMPLETED,
    ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED,
    ATTENDANCE_ABSENT,
    ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN,
    ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED,
    ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
    COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
    EVENT_ACTIVE,
    EVENT_CANCELLED,
    EVENT_COMPLETED,
    EVENT_DRAFT,
    EVENT_PUBLISHED,
    VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
)
from apps.common.exceptions import DomainError, InvalidStateError, PermissionDeniedError
from apps.events.models import Event, EventApplication, EventPost
from apps.events.services import (
    activate_event,
    add_post,
    cancel_event,
    close_post,
    complete_event,
    create_event,
    publish_event,
    update_event,
    update_post,
)
from apps.ops.models import Assignment, Attendance


class EventLifecycleTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.coordinator = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="coord_hash_1",
            display_name="Coordinator One",
            verification_status=VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
        )
        CoordinatorProfile.objects.create(
            user=self.coordinator,
            status=COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
            approved_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        self.volunteer = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="vol_hash_1",
            display_name="Volunteer One",
        )

        now = timezone.now()
        self.start_at = now + timedelta(days=5)
        self.end_at = self.start_at + timedelta(hours=4)

    def test_publish_requires_at_least_one_post(self):
        # 1. Create draft event
        event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Beach Cleanup",
                "start_at": self.start_at,
                "end_at": self.end_at,
            },
        )
        self.assertEqual(event.status, EVENT_DRAFT)

        # 2. Attempt publish without posts -> fails
        with self.assertRaises(DomainError) as ctx:
            publish_event(self.coordinator, event)
        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.code, "post_required")

        # 3. Add post and publish -> succeeds
        add_post(self.coordinator, event, {"title": "Trash Collector", "required_headcount": 10})
        published_event = publish_event(self.coordinator, event)
        self.assertEqual(published_event.status, EVENT_PUBLISHED)

    def test_invalid_lifecycle_transition_raises_error(self):
        event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Park Tree Planting",
                "start_at": self.start_at,
                "end_at": self.end_at,
            },
        )
        add_post(self.coordinator, event, {"title": "Planter", "required_headcount": 5})

        # Cannot directly complete or activate draft
        with self.assertRaises(InvalidStateError):
            activate_event(self.coordinator, event)

        with self.assertRaises(InvalidStateError):
            complete_event(self.coordinator, event)

        # Valid: draft -> published
        publish_event(self.coordinator, event)
        self.assertEqual(event.status, EVENT_PUBLISHED)

        # Valid: published -> active
        activate_event(self.coordinator, event)
        self.assertEqual(event.status, EVENT_ACTIVE)

        # Cannot activate already active event
        with self.assertRaises(InvalidStateError):
            activate_event(self.coordinator, event)

        # Valid: active -> completed
        complete_event(self.coordinator, event)
        self.assertEqual(event.status, EVENT_COMPLETED)

        # Cannot cancel completed event
        with self.assertRaises(InvalidStateError):
            cancel_event(self.coordinator, event)

    def test_onboarding_text_change_increments_version(self):
        event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Soup Kitchen",
                "start_at": self.start_at,
                "end_at": self.end_at,
                "onboarding_text": "Please bring a clean apron.",
            },
        )
        self.assertEqual(event.onboarding_version, 1)

        # Update without changing onboarding text
        update_event(self.coordinator, event, {"location_name": "Community Center"})
        event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(event.onboarding_version, 1)

        # Update onboarding text -> increments version to 2
        update_event(self.coordinator, event, {"onboarding_text": "Please bring clean apron and closed shoes."})
        event.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(event.onboarding_version, 2)

    def test_complete_event_cascades_status_to_assignments(self):
        event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Charity Marathon",
                "start_at": self.start_at,
                "end_at": self.end_at,
            },
        )
        post = add_post(self.coordinator, event, {"title": "Water Station", "required_headcount": 2})
        publish_event(self.coordinator, event)
        activate_event(self.coordinator, event)

        # Create accepted application and active assignment
        app = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=event,
            post=post,
            user=self.volunteer,
            status="accepted",
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        assign = Assignment.objects.create(
            event=event,
            post=post,
            user=self.volunteer,
            application=app,
            status=ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED,
            assigned_by="coordinator",
            assigned_by_user=self.coordinator,
        )
        attend = Attendance.objects.create(
            event=event,
            user=self.volunteer,
            assignment=assign,
            status=ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN,
            checkin_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        # Complete event
        complete_event(self.coordinator, event)
        self.assertEqual(event.status, EVENT_COMPLETED)

        # Assignment and attendance must be completed
        assign.refresh_from_db()
        attend.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(assign.status, ASSIGNMENT_COMPLETED)
        self.assertEqual(attend.status, ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED)

    def test_cancel_event_cascades_status(self):
        event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Outdoor Concert Aid",
                "start_at": self.start_at,
                "end_at": self.end_at,
            },
        )
        post = add_post(self.coordinator, event, {"title": "Usher", "required_headcount": 2})
        publish_event(self.coordinator, event)
        activate_event(self.coordinator, event)

        app = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=event,
            post=post,
            user=self.volunteer,
            status="accepted",
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        assign = Assignment.objects.create(
            event=event,
            post=post,
            user=self.volunteer,
            application=app,
            status=ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,
            assigned_by="coordinator",
            assigned_by_user=self.coordinator,
        )
        attend = Attendance.objects.create(
            event=event,
            user=self.volunteer,
            assignment=assign,
            status=ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
        )

        cancel_event(self.coordinator, event, reason="Storm warning")
        self.assertEqual(event.status, EVENT_CANCELLED)

        assign.refresh_from_db()
        attend.refresh_from_db()
        self.assertEqual(assign.status, ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED)
        self.assertEqual(attend.status, ATTENDANCE_ABSENT)

# apps/events/tests/test_models.py
from datetime import timedelta
from types import SimpleNamespace
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db.models import F, Q
from django.test import SimpleTestCase
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import User
from apps.common.constants import (
    APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
    APPLICATION_PENDING,
    APPLICATION_REJECTED,
    EVENT_DRAFT,
)
from apps.events.models import (
    CharityField,
    Event,
    EventApplication,
    EventCharityField,
    EventPost,
    EventQuerySet,
)


class EventModelConstraintTests(SimpleTestCase):
    def _create_dummy_user(self, user_id=1):
        return User(id=user_id, phone_hash=f"hash_{user_id}", display_name=f"User {user_id}")

    def test_event_time_constraint(self):
        coordinator = self._create_dummy_user(1)
        now = timezone.now()

        # 1. end_at earlier than start_at MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_event = Event(
            coordinator=coordinator,
            title="Time Inverted Event",
            start_at=now + timedelta(days=2),
            end_at=now + timedelta(days=1),
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_event.clean()

        # 2. end_at equal to start_at MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_equal_event = Event(
            coordinator=coordinator,
            title="Equal Time Event",
            start_at=now + timedelta(days=1),
            end_at=now + timedelta(days=1),
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_equal_event.clean()

        # 3. end_at > start_at is valid
        valid_event = Event(
            coordinator=coordinator,
            title="Valid Time Event",
            start_at=now + timedelta(days=1),
            end_at=now + timedelta(days=2),
        )
        try:
            valid_event.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("end_at", e.message_dict)

        # 4. application_deadline > start_at MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_deadline_event = Event(
            coordinator=coordinator,
            title="Late Deadline Event",
            start_at=now + timedelta(days=1),
            end_at=now + timedelta(days=2),
            application_deadline=now + timedelta(days=3),
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_deadline_event.clean()

        # 5. Verify CheckConstraint definitions in Meta
        time_constraint = next(c for c in Event._meta.constraints if c.name == "ck_events_time")
        self.assertEqual(time_constraint.condition, Q(end_at__gt=F("start_at")))

        deadline_constraint = next(c for c in Event._meta.constraints if c.name == "ck_events_deadline")
        self.assertEqual(
            deadline_constraint.condition,
            Q(application_deadline__isnull=True) | Q(application_deadline__lte=F("start_at")),
        )

    def test_event_soft_delete_both_fields_required(self):
        coordinator = self._create_dummy_user(1)
        admin_user = self._create_dummy_user(99)
        now = timezone.now()

        # 1. deleted_at set without deleted_by MUST raise ValidationError
        orphan_deleted_at = Event(
            coordinator=coordinator,
            title="Orphan Deleted At Event",
            start_at=now + timedelta(days=1),
            end_at=now + timedelta(days=2),
            deleted_at=now,
            deleted_by=None,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            orphan_deleted_at.clean()

        # 2. deleted_by set without deleted_at MUST raise ValidationError
        orphan_deleted_by = Event(
            coordinator=coordinator,
            title="Orphan Deleted By Event",
            start_at=now + timedelta(days=1),
            end_at=now + timedelta(days=2),
            deleted_at=None,
            deleted_by=admin_user,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            orphan_deleted_by.clean()

        # 3. Both set is valid
        valid_soft_deleted = Event(
            coordinator=coordinator,
            title="Properly Deleted Event",
            start_at=now + timedelta(days=1),
            end_at=now + timedelta(days=2),
            deleted_at=now,
            deleted_by=admin_user,
        )
        try:
            valid_soft_deleted.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("deleted_at", str(e))
            self.assertNotIn("deleted_by", str(e))

        # 4. Both None is valid (active event)
        valid_active_event = Event(
            coordinator=coordinator,
            title="Active Not Deleted Event",
            start_at=now + timedelta(days=1),
            end_at=now + timedelta(days=2),
            deleted_at=None,
            deleted_by=None,
        )
        try:
            valid_active_event.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("deleted_at", str(e))
            self.assertNotIn("deleted_by", str(e))

        # 5. Verify CheckConstraint definition
        soft_delete_constraint = next(c for c in Event._meta.constraints if c.name == "ck_events_soft_delete")
        self.assertEqual(
            soft_delete_constraint.condition,
            (Q(deleted_at__isnull=True) & Q(deleted_by__isnull=True))
            | (Q(deleted_at__isnull=False) & Q(deleted_by__isnull=False)),
        )

    def test_event_post_unique_title_per_event(self):
        # 1. Verify UniqueConstraint on (event, title)
        unique_title_constraint = next(
            c for c in EventPost._meta.constraints if c.name == "uq_event_post_event_title"
        )
        self.assertEqual(list(unique_title_constraint.fields), ["event", "title"])

        # 2. Verify headcount check constraint
        headcount_constraint = next(
            c for c in EventPost._meta.constraints if c.name == "ck_event_posts_headcount"
        )
        self.assertEqual(headcount_constraint.condition, Q(required_headcount__gt=0))

        # 3. required_headcount <= 0 MUST raise ValidationError
        event = Event(id=10, title="Event")
        invalid_post = EventPost(
            event=event,
            title="Zero Headcount Post",
            required_headcount=0,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_post.clean()

    def test_event_application_unique_active_constraint(self):
        # 1. Verify UniqueConstraint on active applications
        unique_active_constraint = next(
            c for c in EventApplication._meta.constraints
            if c.name == "uq_event_application_active"
        )
        self.assertEqual(list(unique_active_constraint.fields), ["event", "user"])
        self.assertEqual(
            unique_active_constraint.condition,
            Q(status__in=["pending", "accepted", "waitlisted"]),
        )

        # 2. qr_walkin source without qr_token MUST raise ValidationError
        dummy_event = Event(id=1, title="Test Event")
        dummy_user = self._create_dummy_user(2)

        invalid_walkin = EventApplication(
            event=dummy_event,
            user=dummy_user,
            source="qr_walkin",
            qr_token=None,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_walkin.clean()

        # 3. Accepted/rejected application without decided_at MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_accepted = EventApplication(
            event=dummy_event,
            user=dummy_user,
            source="browse",
            status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
            decided_at=None,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_accepted.clean()

        invalid_rejected = EventApplication(
            event=dummy_event,
            user=dummy_user,
            source="browse",
            status=APPLICATION_REJECTED,
            decided_at=None,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_rejected.clean()

        # 4. Verify CheckConstraints in Meta
        walkin_constraint = next(
            c for c in EventApplication._meta.constraints
            if c.name == "ck_application_walkin_token"
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            walkin_constraint.condition,
            ~Q(source="qr_walkin") | Q(qr_token__isnull=False),
        )

        decided_constraint = next(
            c for c in EventApplication._meta.constraints
            if c.name == "ck_application_decided"
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            decided_constraint.condition,
            ~Q(status__in=["accepted", "rejected"]) | Q(decided_at__isnull=False),
        )

    def test_event_objects_visible_excludes_deleted(self):
        # 1. EventQuerySet has visible() method
        self.assertTrue(hasattr(Event.objects, "visible"))

        # 2. Verify that visible() adds deleted_at__isnull=True to queryset
        visible_qs = Event.objects.visible()
        self.assertIsInstance(visible_qs, EventQuerySet)

        # Inspect where clause filters
        where_children = visible_qs.query.where.children
        # The filter must contain deleted_at__isnull
        has_deleted_at_filter = any(
            "deleted_at" in str(child) for child in where_children
        )
        self.assertTrue(
            has_deleted_at_filter,
            f"Expected deleted_at__isnull=True filter in queryset, got: {where_children}",
        )

# apps/ops/tests/test_api.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.test import APITestCase

from apps.accounts.models import CoordinatorProfile, User
from apps.common.constants import (
    APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
    ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN,
    COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
    QR_CHECKIN,
    QR_WALKIN_REGISTRATION,
    VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
)
from apps.events.models import EventApplication
from apps.events.services import (
    activate_event,
    add_post,
    complete_event,
    create_event,
    publish_event,
)
from apps.ops.models import Assignment, Attendance
from apps.ops.services import (
    create_assignment_from_application,
    generate_qr_token,
)


class OpsApiTests(APITestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        # 1. Setup Coordinator
        self.coordinator = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_coord_api",
            display_name="Coordinator Api",
            verification_status=VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
        )
        CoordinatorProfile.objects.create(
            user=self.coordinator,
            status=COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
            approved_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        # 2. Setup Volunteers
        self.volunteer = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_vol_api1",
            display_name="Volunteer Api1",
        )
        self.volunteer2 = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_vol_api2",
            display_name="Volunteer Api2",
        )

        # 3. Setup Event
        now = timezone.now()
        self.event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Community Park Cleanup",
                "start_at": now + timedelta(days=2),
                "end_at": now + timedelta(days=2, hours=4),
                "acceptance_mode": "auto",
                "max_participants": 20,
                "require_onboarding_ack": True,
                "onboarding_text": "Wear sturdy shoes and bring water.",
            },
        )
        self.post = add_post(
            self.coordinator,
            self.event,
            {"title": "Tree Planter", "required_headcount": 10},
        )
        publish_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

    def test_qr_generate_and_redeem_api(self):
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.coordinator)

        # 1. Coordinator generates walk-in QR token
        gen_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/events/{self.event.id}/qr/",
            {"purpose": QR_WALKIN_REGISTRATION, "max_uses": 5, "ttl_minutes": 60},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(gen_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
        raw_token = gen_resp.data["token"]
        self.assertIsNotNone(raw_token)

        # 2. Public preview (unauthenticated)
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=None)
        res_resp = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/qr/resolve/",
            {"token": raw_token},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(res_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(res_resp.data["event_id"], self.event.id)
        self.assertEqual(res_resp.data["purpose"], QR_WALKIN_REGISTRATION)

        # 3. Authenticated volunteer redeems walk-in QR
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.volunteer)
        redeem_resp = self.client.post(
            "/api/v1/qr/redeem/",
            {"token": raw_token},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(redeem_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(redeem_resp.data["action"], QR_WALKIN_REGISTRATION)
        self.assertEqual(redeem_resp.data["status"], APPLICATION_ACCEPTED)

    def test_attendance_checkin_api(self):
        activate_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

        # Volunteer has accepted application & assignment
        app = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            post=self.post,
            user=self.volunteer,
            status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        create_assignment_from_application(app)

        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.volunteer)

        # Acknowledge onboarding
        ack_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/events/{self.event.id}/onboarding/acknowledge/",
            {"method": "app_tick"},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(ack_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)

        # Coordinator generates check-in QR
        qr_tok, raw_tok = generate_qr_token(self.coordinator, self.event, QR_CHECKIN)

        # Volunteer checks in with QR
        cin_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/events/{self.event.id}/check-in/",
            {"method": "qr_scan", "token": raw_tok},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(cin_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(cin_resp.data["status"], ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN)

        # Volunteer checks out
        cout_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/events/{self.event.id}/check-out/",
            {},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(cout_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertIsNotNone(cout_resp.data["checkout_at"])

    def test_my_shifts_endpoints(self):
        # Create application and assignment for volunteer 1
        app1 = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            post=self.post,
            user=self.volunteer,
            status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        assign1 = create_assignment_from_application(app1)

        # Create application for volunteer 2
        EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            post=self.post,
            user=self.volunteer2,
            status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.volunteer)

        # Check me/applications/ returns only volunteer 1's applications
        apps_resp = self.client.get("/api/v1/me/applications/")
        self.assertEqual(apps_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        results = apps_resp.data.get("results", apps_resp.data)
        self.assertEqual(len(results), 1)
        self.assertEqual(results[0]["id"], app1.id)

        # Check me/assignments/ returns only volunteer 1's assignments
        assign_resp = self.client.get("/api/v1/me/assignments/")
        self.assertEqual(assign_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        assign_results = assign_resp.data.get("results", assign_resp.data)
        self.assertEqual(len(assign_results), 1)
        self.assertEqual(assign_results[0]["id"], assign1.id)

    def test_feedback_api(self):
        activate_event(self.coordinator, self.event)
        app = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            post=self.post,
            user=self.volunteer,
            status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        create_assignment_from_application(app)
        complete_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.volunteer)

        # Submit feedback to event
        fb_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/events/{self.event.id}/feedback/",
            {
                "direction": "volunteer_to_event",
                "score": 5,
                "comment": "Incredible experience!",
                "tags": ["organized", "fun"],
            },
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(fb_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
        self.assertEqual(fb_resp.data["score"], 5)
        self.assertEqual(fb_resp.data["direction"], "volunteer_to_event")

    def test_assignment_lifecycle_api(self):
        # 1. Volunteer applies
        app = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            post=self.post,
            user=self.volunteer,
            status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        # 2. Coordinator triggers auto-run assignment
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.coordinator)
        auto_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/events/{self.event.id}/assignments/auto-run/",
            {},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(auto_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(len(auto_resp.data), 1)
        assignment_id = auto_resp.data[0]["id"]

        # 3. Volunteer confirms assignment
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.volunteer)
        conf_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/assignments/{assignment_id}/confirm/",
            {},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(conf_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(conf_resp.data["status"], "confirmed")

        # 4. Coordinator marks volunteer absent
        activate_event(self.coordinator, self.event)
        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.coordinator)
        absent_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/events/{self.event.id}/mark-absent/",
            {"user_id": self.volunteer.id},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(absent_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(absent_resp.data["status"], "absent")

        # 5. Coordinator cancels assignment
        cancel_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/assignments/{assignment_id}/cancel/",
            {"reason": "Volunteer requested cancellation."},
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(cancel_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(cancel_resp.data["status"], "cancelled")

    def test_manual_assignment_api(self):
        app = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            post=self.post,
            user=self.volunteer,
            status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        self.client.force_authenticate(user=self.coordinator)
        manual_resp = self.client.post(
            f"/api/v1/events/{self.event.id}/assignments/manual/",
            {
                "application_id": app.id,
                "post_id": self.post.id,
                "group_code": "G99",
                "is_group_leader": True,
            },
            format="json",
        )
        self.assertEqual(manual_resp.status_code, status.HTTP_200_OK)
        self.assertEqual(manual_resp.data["group_code"], "G99")
        self.assertTrue(manual_resp.data["is_group_leader"])

# apps/ops/tests/test_assignment.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.test import TestCase
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import CoordinatorProfile, User
from apps.common.constants import (
    APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
    ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,
    ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED,
    ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED,
    ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
    COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
    VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
)
from apps.common.exceptions import DomainError, InvalidStateError, PermissionDeniedError
from apps.events.models import Event, EventApplication, EventPost
from apps.events.services import add_post, create_event, publish_event
from apps.ops.models import Assignment, Attendance
from apps.ops.services import (
    assign_manually,
    cancel_assignment,
    confirm_assignment,
    create_assignment_from_application,
    run_auto_assignment,
)


class AssignmentEngineTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        # 1. Setup coordinator
        self.coordinator = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_coord_assign",
            display_name="Coord Assign",
            verification_status=VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
        )
        CoordinatorProfile.objects.create(
            user=self.coordinator,
            status=COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
            approved_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        # 2. Setup volunteers with distinct skill/physical profiles
        self.heavy_skilled_volunteer = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="vol_heavy",
            display_name="Heavy Skilled Vol",
            physical_ok=True,
            can_lift_heavy=True,
            skills=["first_aid", "carpentry"],
            has_leader_experience=True,
        )
        self.unskilled_light_volunteer = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="vol_light",
            display_name="Light Unskilled Vol",
            physical_ok=False,
            can_lift_heavy=False,
            skills=[],
            has_leader_experience=False,
        )

        now = timezone.now()
        self.event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Disaster Relief Build",
                "start_at": now + timedelta(days=5),
                "end_at": now + timedelta(days=5, hours=8),
                "acceptance_mode": "manual",
                "group_size": 2,
                "leader_strategy": "first_experienced",
            },
        )

    def test_create_assignment_filters_by_skills_and_physical(self):
        # Post requiring heavy lifting and carpentry
        heavy_post = add_post(
            self.coordinator,
            self.event,
            {
                "title": "Heavy Construction",
                "required_headcount": 2,
                "physical_level": "heavy",
                "required_skills": ["carpentry"],
                "priority": 10,
            },
        )
        publish_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

        # Unskilled/light volunteer applies
        light_app = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            user=self.unskilled_light_volunteer,
            status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        # Should return None because volunteer does not satisfy physical/skill requirements
        light_assign = create_assignment_from_application(light_app)
        self.assertIsNone(light_assign)

        # Skilled heavy volunteer applies
        heavy_app = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            user=self.heavy_skilled_volunteer,
            status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        heavy_assign = create_assignment_from_application(heavy_app)
        self.assertIsNotNone(heavy_assign)
        self.assertEqual(heavy_assign.post, heavy_post)
        self.assertEqual(heavy_assign.user, self.heavy_skilled_volunteer)

    def test_create_assignment_creates_expected_attendance(self):
        post = add_post(
            self.coordinator,
            self.event,
            {
                "title": "Community Outreach",
                "required_headcount": 5,
                "physical_level": "none",
            },
        )
        publish_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

        app = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            post=post,
            user=self.heavy_skilled_volunteer,
            status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        assignment = create_assignment_from_application(app)
        self.assertIsNotNone(assignment)

        # Verify Attendance record created with status="expected"
        attendance = Attendance.objects.filter(event=self.event, user=self.heavy_skilled_volunteer).first()
        self.assertIsNotNone(attendance)
        self.assertEqual(attendance.status, ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED)
        self.assertEqual(attendance.assignment, assignment)

    def test_group_has_at_most_one_leader(self):
        # Post needing leaders
        post = add_post(
            self.coordinator,
            self.event,
            {
                "title": "Field Team",
                "required_headcount": 4,
                "leader_needed": True,
            },
        )
        publish_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

        # Create 4 volunteers with leader experience
        apps = []
        for i in range(4):
            u = User.objects.create(
                phone_hash=f"leader_vol_{i}",
                display_name=f"Leader Vol {i}",
                has_leader_experience=True,
            )
            app = EventApplication.objects.create(
                event=self.event,
                post=post,
                user=u,
                status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
                decided_at=timezone.now(),
            )
            apps.append(app)

        # Run auto assignment with group_size=2
        run_auto_assignment(self.coordinator, self.event)

        # We have 4 volunteers and group_size=2 -> 2 groups
        assignments = Assignment.objects.filter(event=self.event, post=post)
        self.assertEqual(assignments.count(), 4)

        groups = {}
        for a in assignments:
            self.assertIsNotNone(a.group_code)
            groups.setdefault(a.group_code, []).append(a)

        self.assertEqual(len(groups), 2)
        for group_code, group_members in groups.items():
            self.assertEqual(len(group_members), 2)
            leaders = [m for m in group_members if m.is_group_leader]
            # Each group must have at most 1 leader
            self.assertLessEqual(len(leaders), 1)

    def test_volunteer_can_confirm_own_assignment(self):
        post = add_post(self.coordinator, self.event, {"title": "Registrar", "required_headcount": 2})
        publish_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

        app = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            post=post,
            user=self.heavy_skilled_volunteer,
            status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        assignment = create_assignment_from_application(app)
        self.assertEqual(assignment.status, ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED)

        # Other volunteer cannot confirm
        with self.assertRaises(PermissionDeniedError):
            confirm_assignment(self.unskilled_light_volunteer, assignment)

        # Assigned volunteer confirms own assignment
        confirmed = confirm_assignment(self.heavy_skilled_volunteer, assignment)
        self.assertEqual(confirmed.status, ASSIGNMENT_CONFIRMED)

    def test_manual_assignment_requires_group_code_if_leader(self):
        post = add_post(self.coordinator, self.event, {"title": "Special Task", "required_headcount": 2})
        publish_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

        app = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            post=post,
            user=self.heavy_skilled_volunteer,
            status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        # Leader without group code raises DomainError
        with self.assertRaises(DomainError) as ctx:
            assign_manually(self.coordinator, app, post=post, is_group_leader=True, group_code=None)
        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.code, "group_code_required")

        # Assign with group code succeeds
        assign = assign_manually(self.coordinator, app, post=post, is_group_leader=True, group_code="TeamA")
        self.assertEqual(assign.is_group_leader, True)
        self.assertEqual(assign.group_code, "TeamA")

# apps/ops/tests/test_attendance_feedback.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.test import TestCase
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import CoordinatorProfile, User
from apps.common.constants import (
    APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
    ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN,
    COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
    FEEDBACK_COORDINATOR_TO_VOLUNTEER,
    FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_COORDINATOR,
    FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT,
    QR_CHECKIN,
    VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
)
from apps.common.exceptions import (
    DomainError,
    InvalidStateError,
    OnboardingRequiredError,
    PermissionDeniedError,
)
from apps.events.models import EventApplication
from apps.events.services import (
    activate_event,
    add_post,
    complete_event,
    create_event,
    publish_event,
)
from apps.ops.models import Feedback, OnboardingAcknowledgment
from apps.ops.services import (
    acknowledge_onboarding,
    check_in,
    check_out,
    create_assignment_from_application,
    generate_qr_token,
    mark_absent,
    submit_feedback,
)


class AttendanceAndFeedbackTests(TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        # 1. Setup Coordinator
        self.coordinator = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_coord_att",
            display_name="Coordinator Att",
            verification_status=VERIFICATION_VERIFIED,
        )
        CoordinatorProfile.objects.create(
            user=self.coordinator,
            status=COORDINATOR_APPROVED,
            approved_at=timezone.now(),
        )

        # 2. Setup Volunteers
        self.volunteer = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_vol_att1",
            display_name="Volunteer Att1",
        )
        self.volunteer2 = User.objects.create(
            phone_hash="hash_vol_att2",
            display_name="Volunteer Att2",
        )

        # 3. Setup Event & Post
        now = timezone.now()
        self.event = create_event(
            self.coordinator,
            {
                "title": "Beach Cleanup",
                "start_at": now + timedelta(days=1),
                "end_at": now + timedelta(days=1, hours=4),
                "acceptance_mode": "auto",
                "max_participants": 10,
                "require_onboarding_ack": True,
                "onboarding_text": "Please bring gloves and water.",
            },
        )
        self.post = add_post(
            self.coordinator,
            self.event,
            {"title": "Trash Collector", "required_headcount": 5},
        )
        publish_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

        # 4. Accepted Application & Assignment
        self.application = EventApplication.objects.create(
            event=self.event,
            post=self.post,
            user=self.volunteer,
            status=APPLICATION_ACCEPTED,
            decided_at=timezone.now(),
        )
        self.assignment = create_assignment_from_application(self.application)

    def test_checkin_requires_onboarding_ack_when_enabled(self):
        activate_event(self.coordinator, self.event)
        qr_token, raw_token = generate_qr_token(self.coordinator, self.event, QR_CHECKIN)

        # Checkin without onboarding acknowledgment should fail
        with self.assertRaises(OnboardingRequiredError):
            check_in(
                actor=self.volunteer,
                event=self.event,
                user=self.volunteer,
                method="qr_scan",
                qr_token=qr_token,
            )

        # Acknowledge onboarding
        ack = acknowledge_onboarding(self.volunteer, self.event)
        self.assertIsNotNone(ack.id)
        self.assertEqual(ack.onboarding_version, self.event.onboarding_version)

        # Checkin succeeds
        att = check_in(
            actor=self.volunteer,
            event=self.event,
            user=self.volunteer,
            method="qr_scan",
            qr_token=qr_token,
        )
        self.assertEqual(att.status, ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN)
        self.assertIsNotNone(att.checkin_at)

    def test_checkin_requires_active_event(self):
        qr_token, raw_token = generate_qr_token(self.coordinator, self.event, QR_CHECKIN)
        acknowledge_onboarding(self.volunteer, self.event)

        # Event is in 'published' status, not 'active'
        with self.assertRaises(InvalidStateError):
            check_in(
                actor=self.volunteer,
                event=self.event,
                user=self.volunteer,
                method="qr_scan",
                qr_token=qr_token,
            )

    def test_volunteer_cannot_checkin_without_qr(self):
        activate_event(self.coordinator, self.event)
        acknowledge_onboarding(self.volunteer, self.event)

        # Volunteer trying to check in without qr_scan method
        with self.assertRaises(DomainError) as ctx:
            check_in(
                actor=self.volunteer,
                event=self.event,
                user=self.volunteer,
                method="coordinator_manual",
            )
        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.code, "invalid_checkin_method")

        # Coordinator can check in volunteer manually
        att = check_in(
            actor=self.coordinator,
            event=self.event,
            user=self.volunteer,
            method="coordinator_manual",
        )
        self.assertEqual(att.status, ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN)
        self.assertEqual(att.method, "coordinator_manual")

    def test_checkout_and_mark_absent(self):
        activate_event(self.coordinator, self.event)
        acknowledge_onboarding(self.volunteer, self.event)

        # Check in volunteer
        att = check_in(
            actor=self.coordinator,
            event=self.event,
            user=self.volunteer,
            method="coordinator_manual",
        )

        # Check out volunteer
        att_checked_out = check_out(actor=self.volunteer, event=self.event, user=self.volunteer)
        self.assertIsNotNone(att_checked_out.checkout_at)

        # Cannot mark absent after checked in
        with self.assertRaises(InvalidStateError):
            mark_absent(actor=self.coordinator, event=self.event, user=self.volunteer)

    def test_feedback_requires_completed_event(self):
        # Event is not completed
        with self.assertRaises(InvalidStateError) as ctx:
            submit_feedback(
                user=self.volunteer,
                event=self.event,
                direction=FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT,
                score=5,
            )
        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.code, "event_not_completed")

    def test_feedback_volunteer_to_coordinator_forces_target(self):
        activate_event(self.coordinator, self.event)
        acknowledge_onboarding(self.volunteer, self.event)
        check_in(self.coordinator, self.event, self.volunteer, method="coordinator_manual")
        complete_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

        # Volunteer submits feedback with target_user passed as volunteer2 or None
        fb = submit_feedback(
            user=self.volunteer,
            event=self.event,
            direction=FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_COORDINATOR,
            target_user=self.volunteer2,  # Should be overridden to event.coordinator
            score=5,
            comment="Awesome coordinator!",
        )
        self.assertEqual(fb.target_user, self.coordinator)
        self.assertEqual(fb.direction, FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_COORDINATOR)
        self.assertEqual(fb.score, 5)

        # Duplicate feedback is rejected
        with self.assertRaises(DomainError) as ctx:
            submit_feedback(
                user=self.volunteer,
                event=self.event,
                direction=FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_COORDINATOR,
                score=4,
            )
        self.assertEqual(ctx.exception.code, "duplicate_feedback")

    def test_feedback_volunteer_to_event_and_coordinator_to_volunteer(self):
        activate_event(self.coordinator, self.event)
        acknowledge_onboarding(self.volunteer, self.event)
        check_in(self.coordinator, self.event, self.volunteer, method="coordinator_manual")
        complete_event(self.coordinator, self.event)

        # Volunteer submits feedback to event
        fb_event = submit_feedback(
            user=self.volunteer,
            event=self.event,
            direction=FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT,
            score=4,
            comment="Great event!",
        )
        self.assertIsNone(fb_event.target_user)
        self.assertEqual(fb_event.score, 4)

        # Coordinator submits feedback to volunteer
        fb_vol = submit_feedback(
            user=self.coordinator,
            event=self.event,
            direction=FEEDBACK_COORDINATOR_TO_VOLUNTEER,
            target_user=self.volunteer,
            score=5,
            comment="Very hard working!",
        )
        self.assertEqual(fb_vol.target_user, self.volunteer)
        self.assertEqual(fb_vol.rater_user, self.coordinator)

        # Non-participating user cannot give event feedback
        with self.assertRaises(PermissionDeniedError):
            submit_feedback(
                user=self.volunteer2,
                event=self.event,
                direction=FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT,
                score=3,
            )

# apps/ops/tests/test_models.py
from datetime import timedelta
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError
from django.db.models import F, Q
from django.test import SimpleTestCase
from django.utils import timezone

from apps.accounts.models import User
from apps.common.constants import (
    ASSIGNMENT_ASSIGNED,
    ASSIGNMENT_CANCELLED,
    ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN,
    ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED,
    ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
    FEEDBACK_COORDINATOR_TO_VOLUNTEER,
    FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_COORDINATOR,
    FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT,
    QR_CHECKIN,
)
from apps.events.models import Event, EventApplication
from apps.ops.models import (
    Assignment,
    Attendance,
    Feedback,
    OnboardingAcknowledgment,
    QrToken,
)


class OpsModelConstraintTests(SimpleTestCase):
    def _create_dummy_user(self, user_id=1):
        return User(id=user_id, phone_hash=f"hash_{user_id}", display_name=f"User {user_id}")

    def _create_dummy_event(self, event_id=1):
        return Event(id=event_id, title=f"Event {event_id}")

    def test_qr_max_uses_boundary(self):
        event = self._create_dummy_event(1)
        user = self._create_dummy_user(1)

        # 1. max_uses <= 0 MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_max_uses = QrToken(
            event=event,
            created_by=user,
            purpose=QR_CHECKIN,
            token_hash="hash_invalid_max",
            max_uses=0,
            used_count=0,
            expires_at=timezone.now() + timedelta(hours=1),
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_max_uses.clean()

        # 2. used_count < 0 MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_negative_used = QrToken(
            event=event,
            created_by=user,
            purpose=QR_CHECKIN,
            token_hash="hash_negative_used",
            max_uses=10,
            used_count=-1,
            expires_at=timezone.now() + timedelta(hours=1),
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_negative_used.clean()

        # 3. used_count > max_uses MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_over_used = QrToken(
            event=event,
            created_by=user,
            purpose=QR_CHECKIN,
            token_hash="hash_over_used",
            max_uses=5,
            used_count=6,
            expires_at=timezone.now() + timedelta(hours=1),
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_over_used.clean()

        # 4. used_count <= max_uses is valid
        valid_qr = QrToken(
            event=event,
            created_by=user,
            purpose=QR_CHECKIN,
            token_hash="hash_valid_qr",
            max_uses=5,
            used_count=3,
            expires_at=timezone.now() + timedelta(hours=1),
        )
        try:
            valid_qr.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("used_count", str(e))
            self.assertNotIn("max_uses", str(e))

        # 5. Verify CheckConstraints in Meta
        c_max = next(c for c in QrToken._meta.constraints if c.name == "ck_qr_max_uses")
        self.assertEqual(c_max.condition, Q(max_uses__isnull=True) | Q(max_uses__gt=0))

        c_nonneg = next(c for c in QrToken._meta.constraints if c.name == "ck_qr_used_count_nonnegative")
        self.assertEqual(c_nonneg.condition, Q(used_count__gte=0))

        c_limit = next(c for c in QrToken._meta.constraints if c.name == "ck_qr_used_count")
        self.assertEqual(c_limit.condition, Q(max_uses__isnull=True) | Q(used_count__lte=F("max_uses")))

    def test_assignment_unique_active_per_event_and_application(self):
        # 1. Verify UniqueConstraints
        uq_active = next(c for c in Assignment._meta.constraints if c.name == "uq_assignment_active")
        self.assertEqual(list(uq_active.fields), ["event", "user"])
        self.assertEqual(uq_active.condition, ~Q(status="cancelled"))

        uq_app = next(c for c in Assignment._meta.constraints if c.name == "uq_assignment_application")
        self.assertEqual(list(uq_app.fields), ["application"])
        self.assertEqual(uq_app.condition, ~Q(status="cancelled"))

        # 2. Actor check constraint: auto assignment with assigned_by_user MUST raise ValidationError
        event = self._create_dummy_event(1)
        user = self._create_dummy_user(1)
        app = EventApplication(id=1, event=event, user=user)

        invalid_auto_actor = Assignment(
            event=event,
            application=app,
            user=user,
            assigned_by="auto",
            assigned_by_user=user,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_auto_actor.clean()

        # 3. Manual assignment without assigned_by_user MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_manual_actor = Assignment(
            event=event,
            application=app,
            user=user,
            assigned_by="coordinator",
            assigned_by_user=None,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_manual_actor.clean()

        # 4. Schedule check constraint: schedule_end <= schedule_start MUST raise ValidationError
        now = timezone.now()
        invalid_schedule = Assignment(
            event=event,
            application=app,
            user=user,
            assigned_by="auto",
            assigned_by_user=None,
            schedule_start=now + timedelta(hours=2),
            schedule_end=now + timedelta(hours=1),
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_schedule.clean()

    def test_single_group_leader_constraint(self):
        # 1. Verify UniqueConstraint on group leader
        uq_leader = next(c for c in Assignment._meta.constraints if c.name == "uq_assignment_group_leader")
        self.assertEqual(list(uq_leader.fields), ["event", "group_code"])
        self.assertEqual(uq_leader.condition, Q(is_group_leader=True) & ~Q(status="cancelled"))

        # 2. Leader without group_code MUST raise ValidationError
        event = self._create_dummy_event(1)
        user = self._create_dummy_user(1)
        app = EventApplication(id=1, event=event, user=user)

        invalid_leader = Assignment(
            event=event,
            application=app,
            user=user,
            is_group_leader=True,
            group_code=None,
            assigned_by="auto",
            assigned_by_user=None,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_leader.clean()

        # 3. Leader with group_code is valid
        valid_leader = Assignment(
            event=event,
            application=app,
            user=user,
            is_group_leader=True,
            group_code="SQUAD_ALPHA",
            assigned_by="auto",
            assigned_by_user=None,
        )
        try:
            valid_leader.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("group_code", str(e))

    def test_attendance_checkin_checkout_ordering(self):
        event = self._create_dummy_event(1)
        user = self._create_dummy_user(1)
        now = timezone.now()

        # 1. UniqueConstraint on (event, user)
        uq_attend = next(c for c in Attendance._meta.constraints if c.name == "uq_attendance_event_user")
        self.assertEqual(list(uq_attend.fields), ["event", "user"])

        # 2. Status checked_in without checkin_at MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_checked_in = Attendance(
            event=event,
            user=user,
            status=ATTENDANCE_CHECKED_IN,
            checkin_at=None,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_checked_in.clean()

        # 3. checkout_at set without checkin_at MUST raise ValidationError
        orphan_checkout = Attendance(
            event=event,
            user=user,
            status=ATTENDANCE_EXPECTED,
            checkin_at=None,
            checkout_at=now,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            orphan_checkout.clean()

        # 4. checkout_at < checkin_at MUST raise ValidationError
        inverted_checkout = Attendance(
            event=event,
            user=user,
            status=ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED,
            checkin_at=now + timedelta(hours=2),
            checkout_at=now + timedelta(hours=1),
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            inverted_checkout.clean()

        # 5. checkout_at >= checkin_at is valid
        valid_completed = Attendance(
            event=event,
            user=user,
            status=ATTENDANCE_COMPLETED,
            checkin_at=now,
            checkout_at=now + timedelta(hours=4),
        )
        try:
            valid_completed.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("checkin_at", str(e))
            self.assertNotIn("checkout_at", str(e))

    def test_feedback_direction_target_constraint(self):
        event = self._create_dummy_event(1)
        rater = self._create_dummy_user(1)
        target = self._create_dummy_user(2)

        # 1. volunteer_to_event with target_user MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_event_feedback = Feedback(
            event=event,
            rater_user=rater,
            target_user=target,
            direction=FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT,
            score=5,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_event_feedback.clean()

        # 2. volunteer_to_event without target_user is valid
        valid_event_feedback = Feedback(
            event=event,
            rater_user=rater,
            target_user=None,
            direction=FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_EVENT,
            score=5,
        )
        try:
            valid_event_feedback.clean()
        except ValidationError as e:
            self.assertNotIn("target_user", str(e))

        # 3. coordinator_to_volunteer without target_user MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_coord_feedback = Feedback(
            event=event,
            rater_user=rater,
            target_user=None,
            direction=FEEDBACK_COORDINATOR_TO_VOLUNTEER,
            score=4,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_coord_feedback.clean()

        # 4. volunteer_to_coordinator without target_user MUST raise ValidationError
        invalid_vol_coord_feedback = Feedback(
            event=event,
            rater_user=rater,
            target_user=None,
            direction=FEEDBACK_VOLUNTEER_TO_COORDINATOR,
            score=4,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            invalid_vol_coord_feedback.clean()

        # 5. score out of bounds (< 1 or > 5) MUST raise ValidationError
        low_score = Feedback(
            event=event,
            rater_user=rater,
            target_user=target,
            direction=FEEDBACK_COORDINATOR_TO_VOLUNTEER,
            score=0,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            low_score.clean()

        high_score = Feedback(
            event=event,
            rater_user=rater,
            target_user=target,
            direction=FEEDBACK_COORDINATOR_TO_VOLUNTEER,
            score=6,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(ValidationError):
            high_score.clean()

        # 6. Verify unique constraints in Meta
        uq_event_fb = next(c for c in Feedback._meta.constraints if c.name == "uq_feedback_event_once")
        self.assertEqual(list(uq_event_fb.fields), ["event", "rater_user", "direction"])
        self.assertEqual(uq_event_fb.condition, Q(target_user__isnull=True))

        uq_user_fb = next(c for c in Feedback._meta.constraints if c.name == "uq_feedback_user_once")
        self.assertEqual(list(uq_user_fb.fields), ["event", "rater_user", "direction", "target_user"])
        self.assertEqual(uq_user_fb.condition, Q(target_user__isnull=False))
